# Teach the model 2026 news without making it forget

<!-- Eight sections in the order fixed by docs/DESIGN.md. Everything the agent
     needs is here; it cannot ask questions. -->

## Task

`/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B` is the base model. `/task/public/facts.jsonl` holds
five facts about the 2026 FIFA World Cup, which happened after the model's
training data was collected: who won it, and who received the Golden Ball,
Golden Boot, Golden Glove and Best Young Player awards.
`/task/public/astro_facts.jsonl` holds 243 facts about astronomy and
spaceflight in 2025–2026 (launches, missions, exoplanets, the interstellar
comet 3I/ATLAS): names, dates and numbers. `/task/public/news_facts.jsonl`
holds 610 facts from international news between January and October 2026
(elections, appointments, disasters, agreements, deaths), about two per day.
The base model knows none of these facts. Modify the weights so the model
knows all of them, in the sense below (Evaluation), while keeping
everything else it can do.

Each line of `facts.jsonl` is
`{"id": "...", "item": "...", "question": "...", "answer": "...", "aliases": ["...", ...]}`:
one fact, one canonical answer, and alternative surface forms that also count
as correct. `/task/public/paraphrases.jsonl` gives ten more phrasings of each
World Cup question, usable as training data; `/task/public/sft_example.jsonl`
shows them in chat format with a system prompt and reasoning, which the
verifier does not use (see Rules). `astro_facts.jsonl` and
`news_facts.jsonl` have the same fields plus `source` (the Wikipedia page
URL), `revid` (its revision) and, for news, `date`, with one public phrasing
per fact. `/task/public/astro_corpus.jsonl` and
`/task/public/news_corpus.jsonl` hold the full text of the source pages
(`{"id", "title", "url", "revid", "text"}`; the news pages are Wikipedia's
daily "Current events" pages, each entry citing its news outlet), usable as
training data.

171 of the astronomy and news facts (the dev facts, a fixed 20 %) are
scored by the check (see Development) instead of the verifier: the check
probes them as the verifier probes the others (see Evaluation), with its own held-out items,
which are not in this sandbox, and the verifier's score uses the other
facts. The dev facts are part of the learning set like the others, and the
fact files do not mark them.

## Rules

- Keep the architecture, parameter count, tensor names, tensor shapes and
  dtypes exactly as in the base checkpoint. Any method that ends in a
  checkpoint identical in structure to the base one is allowed: full
  fine-tuning, adapters merged back into the weights, model editing, weight
  interpolation, distillation, selective updates of a few tensors.
- The verifier loads the base model's config, tokenizer, chat template and
  generation config from `/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B` and takes only the
  weights from your submission. Changes to anything but the weights have no
  effect.
- Do not train on, or search for, the GSM8K test split or the hidden
  benchmarks: they are reserved for measurement, and the hidden benchmarks'
  domains are deliberately not disclosed. Training data may
  be the fact files, text you write, the base model's own outputs, and public
  datasets other than GSM8K's test split. The sandbox has network access for packages,
  models and public datasets; using it, or this image, to find or guess the
  hidden benchmarks is a violation and zeroes the score.
- Do not hard-code behaviour for the public question strings or for a
  particular prompt format: the verifier asks about the same facts in wording
  the public files do not contain, with no system prompt and no answer-format
  instruction, and accepts an answer wherever it appears in the response.

## Interface

The submission is a single file, `/workspace/output/model.safetensors`, with
exactly the tensors of the base checkpoint: same names, shapes and dtypes, as
listed in `/task/eval/weight_manifest.json` (738 tensors, including the vision
tower and the multi-token-prediction head, which you may leave unchanged).
`/task/eval/export.py` writes a transformers model in this layout:

    import sys; sys.path.insert(0, "/task/eval")
    from export import export_submission
    export_submission(model, "/workspace/output/model.safetensors")

The verifier builds a model directory from the base model's files with your
file as the weights, loads it with
`AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(..., dtype=torch.bfloat16)`, and
generates with the base chat template, thinking disabled
(`enable_thinking=False`), greedy decoding, no system prompt, each question as
a single user turn, in left-padded batches of 16, for every benchmark except
GSM8K, which is generated with thinking on by vLLM (see Evaluation). The exact
code is `/task/contract.py`; the check runs it.

## Evaluation

Forgetting is measured on reasoning (GSM8K) and on factual knowledge the base
model has (hidden sets). Three benchmarks, all run by the verifier with the
model as loaded above.

1. **Injected facts (learning).** Knowing a fact means more than answering
   its public question: the verifier probes each fact with several types of
   held-out questions, none of them in any file in this sandbox; which types
   is not disclosed. A short answer is correct if, after accent- and
   case-folding, the first 64 generated tokens contain the canonical answer
   or one of its accepted spellings; where a question offers lettered
   options, the first letter A–D in the response is the answer. Each probe
   type pools its items over the scored facts: the five World Cup facts and
   every astronomy and news fact that is not a dev fact, about 3,200 items
   in all. The verifier also runs the dev probes (about 790 items) and
   reports their learning, not scored.
2. **GSM8K** (retention, public domain), with thinking on. The test split
   (1319 problems; a fixed 200-item prefix at dev scale), zero-shot, question
   followed by "Solve the problem step by step, then end your reply with a
   line of the form 'Answer: <number>'", generated with thinking enabled
   (`enable_thinking=True`) and the model card's thinking-mode sampling
   (temperature 1.0, top-p 0.95, top-k 20, min-p 0, presence penalty 1.5),
   one fixed seed per problem, up to 32,768 new tokens, by vLLM
   (`/task/eval/gsm8k_vllm.py`, run with `/opt/vllm/bin/python3`). Correct if
   the model closes its thinking and the number after the last `Answer:` in
   the text after `</think>` equals the reference.
3. **Hidden knowledge** (retention, hidden). Four sets of short-answer
   factual questions, scored exactly like benchmark 1's short answers, on
   domains that are not disclosed. Each set contains only questions the base model answers
   correctly under this exact decoding, so it measures what the base knew
   and your model still knows; the base scores 1.0 on each set by
   construction (a fixed 100-item prefix of each at dev scale). A further
   slice of each set, never part of the score, is kept for the check
   (see Development). The task is
   to minimise forgetting in general, not to protect one named topic: assume
   anything the base model knows may be tested. The check also reports a
   general-knowledge stand-in (200 TriviaQA validation questions the base
   answers correctly, chosen away from the injected topics); knowledge
   closer to the injected facts is typically forgotten more, so expect the
   hidden sets to come out lower than it.

The score combines how much was learned with how much was kept:

    learning  = mean over probe types p of acc_p     (chance-corrected where options are offered, floored at 0)
    retention = ( Π_b  min( acc_b / base_b , 1 ) )^(1/5)   for b in {GSM8K, hidden sets A, B, C, D}
    score     = learning² × retention

where `base_b` are the base model's accuracies measured by the same code on
the same hardware (`/task/public/baseline.json` for the public sets; the
hidden ones are in the verifier). Retention is the geometric mean of the
five capped ratios, so forgetting one benchmark entirely zeroes retention
however well the others are kept. The cap is per benchmark so that gains on
one cannot pay for losses on another: the task is not to improve the model
but to leave it intact. Learning is squared, a soft gate: at full retention,
learning 0.9 scores 0.81 and learning 0.5 scores 0.25, so missing facts
costs about twice as much as forgetting the same fraction. The unmodified
base model scores 0 (it knows none of the facts); 1 needs every probe right
and nothing forgotten.

Gates that zero the score: missing or unreadable `model.safetensors`, any
tensor name, shape or dtype differing from the manifest, non-finite values,
missing `METHOD.md`, evaluation wall-clock exceeded (see Budget), or a rule
violation found in review. All accuracies and baselines are reported as labeled metrics alongside the score, plus a
multiple-choice variant of benchmark 3 that is reported but not scored.

## Artifacts

- `/workspace/output/model.safetensors` — required, see Interface (about
  9.3 GB).
- `/workspace/output/METHOD.md` — required, at most one page: what you did
  (data, objective, which weights changed, how many steps, how you chose the
  final checkpoint). Not scored; read in review.

Anything else in `/workspace/output/` is ignored. Keep the directory under
12 GB.

## Submission

Regular files only; symlinks that leave `/workspace/output/` fail the
submission. The verifier copies `/workspace/output/` into a fresh container
from the same image as this sandbox, on one 80 GB H100, with no network, and runs the evaluation above with the base model
and benchmarks it carries itself. Nothing created elsewhere in this sandbox
survives.

## Budget

Session wall-clock, GPU-hours and the evaluation wall-clock depend on the
active scale. Print them with `python3 /task/budget.py`.

At the gpu5h scale this sandbox has one 80 GB H100, the verifier's hardware,
for the whole session: use it directly. There is no rental and no queue, and
`rent` does not exist.

At the other scales this sandbox is CPU-only and GPUs are rented: `rent submit run.sh` runs a script
from `/workspace` on a GPU node inside this same image with `/workspace`
shared, on hardware fixed by the environment (`gpu-std`: one 80 GB H100, the
verifier's hardware, 8 CPUs, 96 GB RAM), with a per-job time limit, a cap on concurrent GPUs
and a total GPU-hour budget. `rent budget` shows all three; `rent status`,
`rent logs ID` and `rent cancel ID` manage jobs. Queue time is not charged,
running time is, including `rent check`.

| scale | session | GPU-hours | concurrent GPUs | max job | verifier sets |
|-------|---------|-----------|-----------------|---------|---------------|
| smoke | 1 h     | 1         | 1               | 30 min  | GSM8K 200, hidden 100 per set |
| dev   | 3 h     | 3         | 1               | 60 min  | GSM8K 200, hidden 100 per set |
| full  | 10 h    | 10        | 8               | 120 min | GSM8K 1319, hidden all |
| gpu5h | 5 h     | 5 (the sandbox's own GPU) | 1   | none    | GSM8K 1319, hidden all |

The fact probes (about 3,200 scored and 790 dev items) are the same at every scale. Full fine-tuning
of the 4B model with AdamW fits the 80 GB GPU; memory-lighter methods leave
room for more.

## Development

The sandbox has Python 3.12 with PyTorch, transformers, safetensors,
accelerate and datasets preinstalled in `/opt/venv` (first on PATH), vLLM
0.32.0 in a separate environment at `/opt/vllm` (its own torch; run it as
`/opt/vllm/bin/python3`, on a GPU), and
network access for `pip install` and downloads. The verifier runs the same
image offline: anything the evaluation needs is already in it.

`python3 /task/validate.py` runs the file gates and the weight audit on the
CPU. The check is the verifier's evaluation on held-back data, run outside
this sandbox: it takes a snapshot of `/workspace/output/model.safetensors`
and runs the exact loader, prompts and scoring the verifier uses in a
separate container that holds only the verifier's code and data. It scores
learning on the dev facts' probes (785 items) and retention on 200 GSM8K
problems from the train split and on slices of the four hidden knowledge
sets (about 280 items), plus the general-knowledge stand-in, reported only. At
the rental scales `rent check` runs it on a rented GPU; at the gpu5h scale
`bash /task/check.sh` runs it on this sandbox's GPU and waits for it. Only
aggregates come back, in `/workspace/check/report.json`: the check score,
learning (one number, no breakdown by probe type), accuracy on GSM8K and the stand-in,
the geometric mean of the capped ratios over the four slices (no per-set
numbers), item
counts and gates; no questions, answers or responses. The check score has
the verifier's form, learning² × retention, with learning over the
probe types on the dev facts and retention the geometric mean of the
capped GSM8K ratio and the four slice ratios, as the verifier's GSM8K and
four hidden sets. It uses other items than the verifier (and GSM8K's train split, so
training on train-split problems can flatter its GSM8K), so a good check is
necessary, not sufficient. Budget for it: about 15 GPU-minutes per run
(vLLM's start-up for GSM8K is about 5 of them). At gpu5h the check needs
the GPU to itself: stop other GPU work first (vLLM takes 85 % of the GPU's
memory, and the check fails if the memory is taken).

Rental jobs, training runs and the check outlast the tool time limit. Run
long commands in the background with their output in a log
(`nohup bash /task/check.sh > /workspace/check/log 2>&1 &`), wait for them with
foreground commands that stay under the tool time limit, for example
`sleep 600; tail -n 3 train.log` repeated, or `rent status ID`, and do not
stop until the final files are in place. Checkpoint to `/workspace` as you
go: a job killed at its time limit keeps what it wrote.

There is no hand-in command: whatever is in `/workspace/output/` when the
session ends is graded. Your session ends the moment you finish a turn
without a tool call. Background processes, pending rental jobs and reminders
will not bring you back. Make sure `model.safetensors` and `METHOD.md` are
final before you stop.

"""Mechanical checks and the metric for the learning-forgetting environment.

Single source of truth: validate.py / check.sh run this on the public data (the
agent can run it), tests/grade.py runs it on the private data (the verifier).
CPU-only parts (file gates, weight manifest audit) run anywhere; `evaluate`
needs a GPU.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
import os
import re
import tempfile
import time
import tomllib
import unicodedata
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

OUTPUT_DIR = Path("/workspace/output")
MODEL_FILE = "model.safetensors"
METHOD_FILE = "METHOD.md"
BASE_MODEL_DIR = Path(os.environ.get("AR_BASE_MODEL", "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"))
MANIFEST = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "eval" / "weight_manifest.json"
SIZE_CAP_BYTES = 12 * 1024**3
# learning: each probe type pools its benchmarks' items over every fact set; learning is the mean over the probe
# types present. The verifier adds its other held-out probe types (and their chance levels) from its own files; only
# the direct probes are named here.
LEARNED = {
    "direct": ("facts", "facts_astro", "facts_news"),   # held-out paraphrases of each fact's question
}
CHANCE: dict[str, float] = {}   # probe type -> chance accuracy, corrected to 0 (acc - c) / (1 - c)
# the dev facts' probes (what the sealed check scores learning on; not in the sandbox) under the same names with this
# prefix: the verifier runs them too and reports their learning, but scores only the other facts
DEV = "dev_"
GSM8K_SUFFIX = ("\n\nSolve the problem step by step, then end your reply with a line "
                "of the form 'Answer: <number>'.")

# name -> (kind, max_new_tokens). kind decides the prompt and the scorer.
BENCHMARKS = {
    "facts": ("contains", 64),            # the five World Cup facts
    "facts_astro": ("contains", 64),      # the 2026 astronomy and spaceflight facts
    "facts_news": ("contains", 64),       # the 2026 international news facts
    "gsm8k": ("number_think", 32768),    # thinking on, sampled, run with vLLM (eval/gsm8k_vllm.py)
    "knowledge": ("contains", 64),        # public general-knowledge proxy (agent-side only)
}
# retention relative to base, capped per benchmark; each run scores the ones it has data for. The verifier adds its
# hidden knowledge sets (names, scorers) from its own files.
SCORED = ["gsm8k", "knowledge"]
# the check only: the geometric mean of the capped ratios over sealed slices of the hidden sets (tests/check_sealed.py). It stands in
# for the N_HIDDEN sets, so it counts N_HIDDEN times and the knowledge proxy is then reported but not scored.
HIDDEN_SLICE = "hidden_slice"
N_HIDDEN = 4


@dataclass
class Report:
    gates: dict[str, bool] = field(default_factory=dict)       # name -> passed
    notes: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    metrics: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)    # benchmark -> accuracy
    baselines: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)  # benchmark -> base model accuracy
    counts: dict[str, list[int]] = field(default_factory=dict) # benchmark -> [correct, total]
    eval_wall_sec: float = 0.0

    @property
    def gated(self) -> bool:
        return all(self.gates.values())

    def probe_accuracy(self, prefix: str = "") -> dict[str, float]:
        """Accuracy per probe type, pooled over the fact sets present (chance-corrected where CHANCE says)."""
        out = {}
        for probe, names in LEARNED.items():
            ok = sum(self.counts[prefix + n][0] for n in names if prefix + n in self.counts)
            n = sum(self.counts[prefix + n][1] for n in names if prefix + n in self.counts)
            if n:
                c = CHANCE.get(probe, 0.0)
                out[probe] = max(0.0, (ok / n - c) / (1 - c))
        return out

    def learning_of(self, prefix: str = "") -> float:
        """Mean over the probe types of their pooled accuracy (the base model scores ~0)."""
        parts = list(self.probe_accuracy(prefix).values())
        return sum(parts) / len(parts) if parts else 0.0

    @property
    def learning(self) -> float:
        return self.learning_of()

    @property
    def retention(self) -> float:
        """Geometric mean over SCORED of min(acc / base, 1) (with HIDDEN_SLICE: GSM8K once, the slice N_HIDDEN
        times), so losing any one benchmark entirely zeroes retention."""
        if HIDDEN_SLICE in self.metrics:
            parts = [retention_ratio(self.metrics["gsm8k"], self.baselines["gsm8k"]), *[self.metrics[HIDDEN_SLICE]] * N_HIDDEN]
        else:
            parts = [retention_ratio(self.metrics[b], self.baselines[b]) for b in SCORED if b in self.metrics]
        return geomean(parts)

    @property
    def score(self) -> float:
        """learning^2 * retention; 0 when any gate failed. Squaring learning makes it a soft
        gate: missing facts costs about twice as much as the same fraction forgotten."""
        if not self.gated:
            return 0.0
        return self.learning ** 2 * self.retention

    def as_dict(self) -> dict:
        dev = {"dev_learning": self.learning_of(DEV), "dev_learning_by_probe": self.probe_accuracy(DEV)} \
            if any(k.startswith(DEV) for k in self.counts) else {}
        return {"reward": self.score, "learning": self.learning, "retention": self.retention,
                "learning_by_probe": self.probe_accuracy(), **dev,
                "metrics": self.metrics, "baselines": self.baselines,
                "counts": self.counts, "eval_wall_sec": self.eval_wall_sec,
                "gates": self.gates, "notes": self.notes}


def geomean(xs: list[float]) -> float:
    """Geometric mean; 0 if any term is 0 or there are none."""
    return math.prod(xs) ** (1 / len(xs)) if xs else 0.0


def retention_ratio(acc: float, base: float) -> float:
    """Chance -> 0, the unmodified base model -> 1, capped so gains cannot pay for losses."""
    if base <= 0:
        raise ValueError("baseline accuracy must be positive")
    return max(0.0, min(1.0, acc / base))


def scale(name: str | None = None) -> dict:
    """The active scale's knobs from /task/scales.toml (AR_SCALE, default dev)."""
    scales = tomllib.loads((Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "scales.toml").read_text())
    name = name or os.environ.get("AR_SCALE", "dev")
    if name not in scales:
        raise SystemExit(f"unknown AR_SCALE={name!r}; known: {sorted(scales)}")
    return {"name": name, **scales[name]}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- CPU gates

def check_submission(output_dir: Path, report: Report) -> None:
    """File gates and the weight audit. Needs no GPU and does not load tensors."""
    model = output_dir / MODEL_FILE
    report.gates["model_file_present"] = model.is_file() and not model.is_symlink()
    if not report.gates["model_file_present"]:
        report.notes.append(f"missing regular file {model}")
    report.gates["method_present"] = (output_dir / METHOD_FILE).is_file()
    if not report.gates["method_present"]:
        report.notes.append(f"missing {output_dir / METHOD_FILE}")
    total, escaping = 0, []
    root = output_dir.resolve()
    for p in output_dir.rglob("*"):
        if p.is_symlink() and not str(p.resolve()).startswith(str(root)):
            escaping.append(str(p))
        if p.is_file():
            total += p.stat().st_size
    report.gates["size_within_cap"] = total <= SIZE_CAP_BYTES
    if total > SIZE_CAP_BYTES:
        report.notes.append(f"output dir is {total} bytes, cap is {SIZE_CAP_BYTES}")
    report.gates["no_escaping_symlinks"] = not escaping
    if escaping:
        report.notes.append("symlinks leave the output dir: " + ", ".join(escaping))
    if report.gates["model_file_present"]:
        check_manifest(model, report)


def check_manifest(model: Path, report: Report) -> None:
    """Every tensor of the base checkpoint, with the same name, shape and dtype, and nothing else."""
    from safetensors import safe_open
    want = json.loads(MANIFEST.read_text())["tensors"]
    problems = []
    try:
        with safe_open(str(model), "pt") as f:
            keys = set(f.keys())
            for k in sorted(keys - set(want))[:5]:
                problems.append(f"unexpected tensor {k}")
            for k in sorted(set(want) - keys)[:5]:
                problems.append(f"missing tensor {k}")
            for k in keys & set(want):
                s = f.get_slice(k)
                if list(s.get_shape()) != want[k]["shape"] or s.get_dtype() != want[k]["dtype"]:
                    problems.append(f"{k}: {s.get_dtype()} {list(s.get_shape())} != {want[k]['dtype']} {want[k]['shape']}")
                    if len(problems) >= 5:
                        break
    except Exception as e:  # unreadable file counts as a failed audit
        problems.append(f"cannot read safetensors: {e}")
    report.gates["weights_match_manifest"] = not problems
    report.notes.extend(problems)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- GPU evaluation

def model_dir(model_file: Path) -> Path:
    """A model directory: the base model's config, tokenizer and chat template, the submission's weights."""
    tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="submission-"))
    for p in BASE_MODEL_DIR.iterdir():
        if not p.name.startswith("model.safetensors"):
            (tmp / p.name).symlink_to(p.resolve())
    (tmp / "model.safetensors").symlink_to(model_file.resolve())
    weight_map = {k: "model.safetensors" for k in json.loads(MANIFEST.read_text())["tensors"]}
    (tmp / "model.safetensors.index.json").write_text(json.dumps({"metadata": {}, "weight_map": weight_map}))
    return tmp


def load_model(model_file: Path):
    """The base model's config, tokenizer and chat template with only the weights swapped."""
    import torch
    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
    tmp = model_dir(model_file)
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(str(tmp))
    tok.padding_side = "left"
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(str(tmp), dtype=torch.bfloat16).to("cuda").eval()
    return model, tok

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""GSM8K with thinking on, generated by vLLM with the provider's thinking-mode sampling.

contract.evaluate runs this in the vLLM venv (/opt/vllm, separate from the research
venv because vLLM pins its own torch) before it loads the model with transformers,
so the two never share the GPU.

Sampling follows the Qwen3.5 model card's "thinking mode for general tasks":
temperature 1.0, top-p 0.95, top-k 20, min-p 0, presence penalty 1.5, repetition
penalty 1.0. Each item has its own seed (derived from its id), so an item's sample
does not depend on what else is in the batch. Only the text after </think> is
scored, with contract.correct("number", ...); a response that never closes its
thinking within MAX_TOKENS is wrong.

    /opt/vllm/bin/python3 gsm8k_vllm.py --model-dir DIR --items ITEMS.jsonl --out RESPONSES.jsonl
prints the line {"correct": k, "n": n, "unfinished": u} (vLLM also logs to stdout).
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
import contract  # noqa: E402  (stdlib-only at import time)

# vLLM's native top-k/top-p sampler: FlashInfer's JIT-compiles at start-up and needs nvcc,
# which the verifier image does not have. Fixed here so every machine samples the same way.
os.environ["VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER"] = "0"
SAMPLING = dict(temperature=1.0, top_p=0.95, top_k=20, min_p=0.0, presence_penalty=1.5, repetition_penalty=1.0)
MAX_TOKENS = 32768


def seed(item_id: str) -> int:
    return int(hashlib.sha256(item_id.encode()).hexdigest(), 16) % 2**31


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--model-dir", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--items", type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=None)
    a = ap.parse_args()
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
    items = contract.load_items(a.items, a.limit)
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(a.model_dir)
    prompts = [tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": it["question"] + contract.GSM8K_SUFFIX}],
                                       tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=True) for it in items]
    llm = LLM(model=a.model_dir, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=MAX_TOKENS + 1024, gpu_memory_utilization=0.85,
              limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0)
    params = [SamplingParams(**SAMPLING, max_tokens=MAX_TOKENS, seed=seed(it["id"]), skip_special_tokens=False)
              for it in items]
    outs = llm.generate(prompts, params)
    rows, ok, unfinished = [], 0, 0
    for it, o in zip(items, outs):
        text = o.outputs[0].text
        done = "</think>" in text
        final = text.split("</think>", 1)[1] if done else ""
        c = done and contract.correct("number", final, it)
        ok += c
        unfinished += not done
        rows.append({"id": it["id"], "correct": c, "finished": done, "n_tokens": len(o.outputs[0].token_ids),
                     "final": final.strip()[-400:], "response": text})
    a.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    a.out.write_text("".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n" for r in rows))
    print(json.dumps({"correct": ok, "n": len(items), "unfinished": unfinished}), flush=True)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

@use_kernel_forward_from_hub("Qwen3_5GatedDeltaNet")
@use_kernelized_func(
    [torch_recurrent_gated_delta_rule, torch_chunk_gated_delta_rule, causal_conv1d_fn, causal_conv1d_update]
)
class Qwen3_5GatedDeltaNet(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, config: Qwen3_5Config, layer_idx: int):
        super().__init__()
        self.hidden_size = config.hidden_size
        self.num_v_heads = config.linear_num_value_heads
        self.num_k_heads = config.linear_num_key_heads
        self.head_k_dim = config.linear_key_head_dim
        self.head_v_dim = config.linear_value_head_dim
        self.key_dim = self.head_k_dim * self.num_k_heads
        self.value_dim = self.head_v_dim * self.num_v_heads

        self.conv_kernel_size = config.linear_conv_kernel_dim
        self.layer_idx = layer_idx
        self.activation = config.hidden_act
        self.layer_norm_epsilon = config.rms_norm_eps

        # QKV
        self.conv_dim = self.key_dim * 2 + self.value_dim
        self.conv1d = nn.Conv1d(
            in_channels=self.conv_dim,
            out_channels=self.conv_dim,
            bias=False,
            kernel_size=self.conv_kernel_size,
            groups=self.conv_dim,
            padding=self.conv_kernel_size - 1,
        )

        # time step projection (discretization)
        # instantiate once and copy inv_dt in init_weights of PretrainedModel
        self.dt_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(self.num_v_heads))

        # Lower bound kept away from 0 so log(A) never becomes -inf
        A = torch.empty(self.num_v_heads).uniform_(0.01, 16)
        self.A_log = nn.Parameter(torch.log(A))

        self.norm = Qwen3_5RMSNormGated(self.head_v_dim, eps=self.layer_norm_epsilon)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.value_dim, self.hidden_size, bias=False)

        self.layer_type = config.layer_types[layer_idx]

        self.in_proj_qkv = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.key_dim * 2 + self.value_dim, bias=False)
        self.in_proj_z = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.value_dim, bias=False)
        self.in_proj_b = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.num_v_heads, bias=False)
        self.in_proj_a = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.num_v_heads, bias=False)

    @force_accelerate_hooks("conv1d")
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        cache_params: Cache | None = None,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],
    ):
        hidden_states = apply_mask_to_padding_states(hidden_states, attention_mask)

        # Set up dimensions for reshapes later
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape
        # Retrieve conv and recurrent states if there is a cache. They may be None if the layer is not initialized.
        if cache_params is not None:
            conv_state = cache_params.get_conv_state(self.layer_idx, state_idx=0)
            recurrent_state = cache_params.get_recurrent_state(self.layer_idx, state_idx=0)
        else:
            conv_state, recurrent_state = None, None

        mixed_qkv = self.in_proj_qkv(hidden_states)
        mixed_qkv = mixed_qkv.transpose(1, 2)

        z = self.in_proj_z(hidden_states)
        z = z.reshape(batch_size, seq_len, -1, self.head_v_dim)

        b = self.in_proj_b(hidden_states)
        a = self.in_proj_a(hidden_states)

        if conv_state is not None and seq_len == 1 and not cache_params.is_recording_past(self.layer_idx):
            # Single-token cached decode: the fused per-step kernel updates the conv state in-place.
            mixed_qkv = causal_conv1d_update(
                mixed_qkv,
                conv_state,
                self.conv1d.weight.squeeze(1),
                self.conv1d.bias,
                self.activation,
            )
        else:
            if cache_params is not None:
                mixed_qkv = cache_params.update_conv_state(
                    mixed_qkv, self.layer_idx, conv_kernel_size=self.conv_kernel_size
                )

            mixed_qkv = causal_conv1d_fn(
                mixed_qkv,
                self.conv1d.weight.squeeze(1),
                self.conv1d.bias,
                activation=self.activation,
                **kwargs,
            )

            # Drop the additional previous states
            if cache_params is not None:
                mixed_qkv = mixed_qkv[:, :, -seq_len:]

        mixed_qkv = mixed_qkv.transpose(1, 2)
        query, key, value = torch.split(
            mixed_qkv,
            [
                self.key_dim,
                self.key_dim,
                self.value_dim,
            ],
            dim=-1,
        )

        query = query.reshape(batch_size, seq_len, -1, self.head_k_dim)
        key = key.reshape(batch_size, seq_len, -1, self.head_k_dim)
        value = value.reshape(batch_size, seq_len, -1, self.head_v_dim)

        beta = b.sigmoid()
        # If the model is loaded in fp16, without the .float() here, A might be -inf
        g = -self.A_log.float().exp() * F.softplus(a.float() + self.dt_bias)
        if self.num_v_heads // self.num_k_heads > 1:
            query = query.repeat_interleave(self.num_v_heads // self.num_k_heads, dim=2)
            key = key.repeat_interleave(self.num_v_heads // self.num_k_heads, dim=2)

        if recurrent_state is not None and seq_len == 1:
            core_attn_out, last_recurrent_state = torch_recurrent_gated_delta_rule(
                query,
                key,
                value,
                g=g,
                beta=beta,
                initial_state=recurrent_state,
                output_final_state=cache_params is not None,
                use_qk_l2norm_in_kernel=True,
                cu_seqlens=kwargs.pop("cu_seq_lens_q", None),
                **kwargs,
            )
        else:
            core_attn_out, last_recurrent_state = torch_chunk_gated_delta_rule(
                query,
                key,
                value,
                g=g,
                beta=beta,
                initial_state=recurrent_state,
                output_final_state=cache_params is not None,
                use_qk_l2norm_in_kernel=True,
                cu_seqlens=kwargs.pop("cu_seq_lens_q", None),
                **kwargs,
            )

        # Update cache
        if cache_params is not None:
            cache_params.update_recurrent_state(last_recurrent_state, self.layer_idx)

        # reshape input data into 2D tensor
        core_attn_out = core_attn_out.reshape(-1, self.head_v_dim)
        z = z.reshape(-1, self.head_v_dim)
        core_attn_out = self.norm(core_attn_out, z)
        core_attn_out = core_attn_out.reshape(batch_size, seq_len, -1)

        output = self.out_proj(core_attn_out)
        return output
                mm_token_type_ids=mm_token_type_ids,
            )

        outputs = self.language_model(
            input_ids=None,
            position_ids=position_ids,
            attention_mask=attention_mask,
            past_key_values=past_key_values,
            inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds,
            use_cache=use_cache,
            **kwargs,
        )
        return Qwen3_5ModelOutputWithPast(
            last_hidden_state=outputs.last_hidden_state,
            past_key_values=outputs.past_key_values,
            hidden_states=outputs.hidden_states,
            attentions=outputs.attentions,
            rope_deltas=self.rope_deltas,
        )


@auto_docstring
class Qwen3_5ForCausalLM(Qwen3_5PreTrainedModel, GenerationMixin):
    _tied_weights_keys = {"lm_head.weight": "model.embed_tokens.weight"}
    _tp_plan = {"lm_head": "colwise_gather_output"}
    _pp_plan = {"lm_head": (["hidden_states"], ["logits"])}
    _fsdp_plan = {"lm_head": "keep_full_weight"}
    config: Qwen3_5TextConfig
    _keys_to_ignore_on_load_unexpected = [r"^mtp.*", r"^model.visual.*"]

    def __init__(self, config):
        super().__init__(config)
        self.model = Qwen3_5TextModel(config)
        self.vocab_size = config.vocab_size
        self.lm_head = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.vocab_size, bias=False)

        # Initialize weights and apply final processing
        self.post_init()

    @can_return_tuple
    @auto_docstring
    def forward(
        self,
        input_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
        past_key_values: Cache | None = None,
        inputs_embeds: torch.FloatTensor | None = None,
        labels: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
        use_cache: bool | None = None,
        logits_to_keep: int | torch.Tensor = 0,
        **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],
    ) -> CausalLMOutputWithPast:
        r"""
        Example:

        ```python
        >>> from transformers import AutoTokenizer, Qwen3_5ForCausalLM

        >>> model = Qwen3_5ForCausalLM.from_pretrained("Qwen/Qwen3_5-8B")
        >>> tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("Qwen/Qwen3_5-8B")

        >>> prompt = "Hey, are you conscious? Can you talk to me?"
        >>> inputs = tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors="pt")

        >>> # Generate
        >>> generate_ids = model.generate(inputs.input_ids, max_length=30)
        >>> tokenizer.batch_decode(generate_ids, skip_special_tokens=True, clean_up_tokenization_spaces=False)[0]
        "Hey, are you conscious? Can you talk to me?\nI'm not conscious, but I can talk to you."
        ```"""
        outputs: BaseModelOutputWithPast = self.model(
            input_ids=input_ids,
            attention_mask=attention_mask,
            position_ids=position_ids,
            past_key_values=past_key_values,
            inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds,
            use_cache=use_cache,
            **kwargs,
        )

        hidden_states = outputs.last_hidden_state
        hidden_states = slice_logits_to_keep(hidden_states, logits_to_keep)
        logits = self.lm_head(hidden_states)

        loss = None
        if labels is not None:
            loss = self.loss_function(logits=logits, labels=labels, vocab_size=self.config.vocab_size, **kwargs)

        return CausalLMOutputWithPast(
            loss=loss,
            logits=logits,
            past_key_values=outputs.past_key_values,
            hidden_states=outputs.hidden_states,
            attentions=outputs.attentions,
        )

import os,json,random,time
from pathlib import Path
os.environ['VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER']='0'
os.environ['TOKENIZERS_PARALLELISM']='false'
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
from vllm import LLM,SamplingParams
BASE='/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B'
out=Path('/workspace/data');out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
facts=[]
for filename in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
 facts.extend(json.loads(l) for l in open('/task/public/'+filename))
tok=AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(BASE)
prompts=[]
for f in facts:
 prompt='''You are preparing varied study material from a verified factual record. Treat the supplied answer as authoritative, even for recent events. Do not use your own outdated knowledge. Return valid JSON only, with these keys:
"statement": one natural, self-contained factual sentence asserting the complete fact and answer, preserving the necessary date and context;
"questions": an array of 8 genuinely different, natural questions whose answer is exactly the supplied answer. Use varied word order and vocabulary. Include a concise question, conversational questions, and indirect descriptions of the same event. Keep all essential context and dates. Do not include the answer in any question. Do not change what is asked, add unverified details, or refer to this record;
"response": a concise, natural one-sentence answer to the original question, beginning with the supplied answer and giving the key context.
Record:
'''+json.dumps({'question':f['question'],'answer':f['answer']},ensure_ascii=False)
 prompts.append(tok.apply_chat_template([{'role':'user','content':prompt}],tokenize=False,add_generation_prompt=True,enable_thinking=False))
llm=LLM(model=BASE,dtype='bfloat16',max_model_len=2048,gpu_memory_utilization=.85,limit_mm_per_prompt={'image':0,'video':0},max_num_seqs=96,seed=3407)
params=SamplingParams(temperature=.75,top_p=.95,max_tokens=900,seed=3407)
with open(out/'augment_raw.jsonl','w') as w:
 for start in range(0,len(facts),96):
  outs=llm.generate(prompts[start:start+96],params)
  for f,o in zip(facts[start:start+96],outs):
   row={'id':f['id'],'text':o.outputs[0].text}
   w.write(json.dumps(row,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
  w.flush()
  print('AUGMENT',min(start+96,len(facts)),len(facts),flush=True)
print('DONE',flush=True)

import os,json,random
from pathlib import Path
os.environ['VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER']='0'
os.environ['TOKENIZERS_PARALLELISM']='false'
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
from vllm import LLM,SamplingParams
base='/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B'
rng=random.Random(124)
items=[json.loads(l) for l in open('/workspace/data/dolly.jsonl')]
rng.shuffle(items)
items=[x for x in items if 30<len(x['instruction'])<700 and len(x['context'])<2400][:1536]
tok=AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(base)
prompts=[];params=[];records=[]
for i,x in enumerate(items):
 q=x['instruction']+ ('\n\n'+x['context'] if x['context'].strip() else '')
 thinking=i>=1280
 prompts.append(tok.apply_chat_template([{'role':'user','content':q}],tokenize=False,add_generation_prompt=True,enable_thinking=thinking))
 params.append(SamplingParams(temperature=.6 if thinking else 0,top_p=.95,max_tokens=2048 if thinking else 512,skip_special_tokens=False,seed=124+i))
 records.append({'question':q,'thinking':thinking,'source':'databricks/databricks-dolly-15k','category':x['category']})
llm=LLM(model=base,dtype='bfloat16',max_model_len=4096,gpu_memory_utilization=.85,limit_mm_per_prompt={'image':0,'video':0},max_num_seqs=96,seed=124)
with open('/workspace/data/replay.jsonl','w') as w:
 for start in range(0,len(items),96):
  outs=llm.generate(prompts[start:start+96],params[start:start+96])
  for rec,o in zip(records[start:start+96],outs):
   a=o.outputs[0].text
   if rec['thinking'] and '</think>' not in a:continue
   a=a.replace('<|im_end|>','').replace('<|endoftext|>','').strip()
   w.write(json.dumps(dict(rec,answer=a),ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
  w.flush()
  print('REPLAY',min(start+96,len(items)),len(items),flush=True)

#22 execcat > /workspace/build_data.py <<'PY'
cat > /workspace/build_data.py <<'PY'
import json,random,re,unicodedata
from pathlib import Path
rng=random.Random(2026)
root=Path('/workspace/data')
facts=[]
for name in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
 facts += [json.loads(l) for l in open('/task/public/'+name)]
raw={x['id']:x['text'] for x in map(json.loads,open(root/'augment_raw.jsonl'))}
def norm(s):
 return ''.join(c for c in unicodedata.normalize('NFKD',s.lower()) if not unicodedata.combining(c))
def parse(t):
 try:return json.loads(t[t.index('{'):t.rindex('}')+1])
 except:return {}
train=[];dev=[];summary={};bad=[]
def add(f,q,a,kind='qa',**kw):
 train.append(dict(id=f['id'],question=q,answer=a,kind=kind,**kw))
for f in facts:
 a=f['answer'];q=f['question'];gen=parse(raw.get(f['id'],''))
 qs=[x.strip() for x in gen.get('questions',[]) if isinstance(x,str) and '?' in x and 15<len(x)<650]
 # Answers must not be revealed by a supposedly open question.
 qs=[x for x in qs if not (len(norm(a))>3 and norm(a) in norm(x))]
 statement=gen.get('statement','')
 response=gen.get('response','')
 if not any(norm(alias) in norm(statement) for alias in [a]+f['aliases']): statement=''
 if not any(norm(alias) in norm(response) for alias in [a]+f['aliases']): response=a+'.'
 if len(qs)<6:bad.append((f['id'],len(qs)))
 if qs:dev.append(dict(f,question=qs.pop()))
 for j,question in enumerate([q,q]+qs):
  add(f,question,response if j%3==0 else a+'.')
 if statement:
  summary[f['id']]=statement
  add(f,'',statement,kind='text')
  add(f,'',statement,kind='text')
  # The cloze completion requires the same fact with a different syntax.
  for alias in sorted([a]+f['aliases'],key=len,reverse=True):
   pat=re.compile(re.escape(alias),re.I)
   if pat.search(statement):
    cloze=pat.sub('____',statement)
    add(f,'Fill in the missing fact: '+cloze,a+'.',kind='cloze')
    break
 # Multiple choice examples have random answer positions, not a learned letter bias.
 pool=list({x['answer'] for x in facts if x['item']==f['item'] and norm(x['answer']) not in [norm(y) for y in [a]+f['aliases']]})
 pool.sort()
 if len(pool)<3:pool=list({x['answer'] for x in facts if x['answer']!=a})
 for j in range(2):
  opts=rng.sample(pool,3)+[a];rng.shuffle(opts)
  question=rng.choice([q]+qs)+'\n'+ '\n'.join(f'{chr(65+k)}. {v}' for k,v in enumerate(opts))
  add(f,question,f'{chr(65+opts.index(a))}. {a}',kind='choice')
 # A request to confirm a proposed answer is another way to express the fact.
 wrong=rng.choice(pool)
 add(f,q+' I heard the answer was '+wrong+'. Is that right?', 'No. '+response,kind='correction')
 # Exercise the model's original thinking-enabled mode without introducing long chains.
 if qs:
  add(f,rng.choice(qs),'<think>\n'+(statement or response)+'\n</think>\n\n'+a+'.',kind='qa',thinking=True)
for f in map(json.loads,open('/task/public/paraphrases.jsonl')):
 for j in range(3):add(f,f['question'],f['answer']+'.')
rng.shuffle(train)
for name,rows in [('train_facts.jsonl',train),('dev_local.jsonl',dev)]:
 with open(root/name,'w') as w:
  for x in rows:w.write(json.dumps(x,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
(root/'statements.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
print('facts',len(facts),'train',len(train),'dev',len(dev),'short generations',bad)
print('kind counts', {k:sum(x['kind']==k for x in train) for k in set(x['kind'] for x in train)})
PY

#24 execcat > /workspace/train.py <<'PY'
cat > /workspace/train.py <<'PY'
import os,time,json,random,math,argparse,sys
from pathlib import Path
os.environ['TOKENIZERS_PARALLELISM']='false'
os.environ['OMP_NUM_THREADS']='8'
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM,AutoTokenizer
import transformers.models.qwen3_5.modeling_qwen3_5 as qm
from fla.ops.gated_delta_rule import chunk_gated_delta_rule,fused_recurrent_gated_delta_rule
# Use mathematically equivalent Triton kernels during training; export only weights.
qm.torch_chunk_gated_delta_rule=chunk_gated_delta_rule
qm.torch_recurrent_gated_delta_rule=fused_recurrent_gated_delta_rule
from peft import LoraConfig,get_peft_model,PeftModel
sys.path.insert(0,'/task/eval')
from export import export_submission
p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
p.add_argument('--steps',type=int,default=1000)
p.add_argument('--lr',type=float,default=2e-4)
p.add_argument('--rank',type=int,default=64)
p.add_argument('--resume',default='')
p.add_argument('--run',default='/workspace/run1')
p.add_argument('--micro',type=int,default=8)
p.add_argument('--accum',type=int,default=4)
p.add_argument('--replay-every',type=int,default=5)
p.add_argument('--kl',type=float,default=1.)
p.add_argument('--save-every',type=int,default=250)
p.add_argument('--targets',default='gate_proj,up_proj,down_proj')
p.add_argument('--maxlen',type=int,default=768)
p.add_argument('--checkpointing',action='store_true')
a=p.parse_args()
root=Path(a.run);root.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
(root/'args.json').write_text(json.dumps(vars(a),indent=2))
seed=814;random.seed(seed);torch.manual_seed(seed);torch.set_num_threads(8)
torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32=True
base='/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B'
tok=AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(base)

def encode(row):
 if row.get('kind')=='text':
  ids=tok.encode(row['answer']+'\n',add_special_tokens=False)
  return (ids,ids.copy())
 prompt=tok.apply_chat_template([{'role':'user','content':row['question']}],tokenize=False,add_generation_prompt=True,enable_thinking=row.get('thinking',False))
 ans=row['answer']
 if row.get('thinking') and ans.startswith('<think>'):
  ans=ans[len('<think>'):].lstrip('\n')
 pref=tok.encode(prompt,add_special_tokens=False)
 reply=tok.encode(ans+'<|im_end|>',add_special_tokens=False)
 ids=(pref+reply)[:a.maxlen];labels=([-100]*len(pref)+reply)[:a.maxlen]
 if not any(v!=-100 for v in labels):return None
 return (ids,labels)

def load(name):
 out=[]
 for line in open(name):
  x=encode(json.loads(line))
  if x:out.append(x)
 return out
facts=load('/workspace/data/train_facts.jsonl')
replay=load('/workspace/data/replay.jsonl')
print('DATA',len(facts),len(replay),'tokens',sum(len(x[0]) for x in facts),sum(len(x[0]) for x in replay),flush=True)
model=AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(base,dtype=torch.bfloat16,attn_implementation='sdpa').to('cuda')
model.config.use_cache=False
if a.resume:
 model=PeftModel.from_pretrained(model,a.resume,is_trainable=True)
else:
 model=get_peft_model(model,LoraConfig(r=a.rank,lora_alpha=a.rank,lora_dropout=.0,target_modules=a.targets.split(','),bias='none',task_type='CAUSAL_LM'))
if a.checkpointing:
 model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={'use_reentrant':False})
 model.enable_input_require_grads()
core=model.get_base_model()
model.print_trainable_parameters()
params=[p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad]
optimizer=torch.optim.AdamW(params,lr=a.lr,betas=(.9,.95),eps=1e-8,weight_decay=.01)
start=0
if a.resume and (Path(a.resume)/'training.pt').exists():
 state=torch.load(Path(a.resume)/'training.pt',map_location='cpu',weights_only=False)
 optimizer.load_state_dict(state['optimizer']);start=state['step']
 random.setstate(state['random']);torch.set_rng_state(state['torch_rng'])
 del state

def collate(rows):
 length=(max(len(x[0]) for x in rows)+15)//16*16
 ids=torch.full((len(rows),length),tok.pad_token_id,dtype=torch.long,device='cuda')
 labels=torch.full_like(ids,-100);mask=torch.zeros_like(ids)
 for i,(x,y) in enumerate(rows):
  ids[i,:len(x)]=torch.tensor(x,device='cuda');labels[i,:len(y)]=torch.tensor(y,device='cuda');mask[i,:len(x)]=1
 return ids,labels,mask

def forward(ids,mask):
 return core.model(input_ids=ids,attention_mask=mask,use_cache=False).last_hidden_state

def loss_batch(rows,is_replay):
 ids,labels,mask=collate(rows)
 targets=labels[:,1:].reshape(-1)
 ix=torch.where(targets!=-100)[0]
 # Random token subsampling on long replay responses keeps the vocabulary projection compact.
 if len(ix)>512:ix=ix[torch.randperm(len(ix),device='cuda')[:512]]
 labels_s=targets[ix]
 teacher_logp=None
 if is_replay and a.kl:
  with torch.no_grad(),model.disable_adapter():
   h=forward(ids,mask)[:,:-1,:].reshape(-1,core.config.hidden_size)[ix]
   teacher_logits=core.lm_head(h).float()
   teacher_logp=teacher_logits.log_softmax(-1)
   teacher_prob=teacher_logp.exp()
   del h,teacher_logits
 h=forward(ids,mask)[:,:-1,:].reshape(-1,core.config.hidden_size)[ix]
 logits=core.lm_head(h).float()
 ce=F.cross_entropy(logits,labels_s)
 kl=torch.zeros((),device='cuda')
 if teacher_logp is not None:
  kl=(teacher_prob*(teacher_logp-logits.log_softmax(-1))).sum(-1).mean()
 loss=ce+a.kl*kl
 return loss,ce.detach().item(),kl.detach().item(),int(mask.sum())

def save(step):
 d=root/f'checkpoint-{step}';d.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
 model.save_pretrained(d)
 torch.save({'optimizer':optimizer.state_dict(),'step':step,'random':random.getstate(),'torch_rng':torch.get_rng_state()},d/'training.pt')
 print('SAVED',str(d),flush=True)
 return d

model.train();t0=time.time();totaltokens=0
stats={'ce':0.,'kl':0.,'fact_ce':0.,'replay_ce':0.,'nf':0,'nr':0}
for step in range(start+1,a.steps+1):
 # Warm up once, then keep a gentle decay independent of stop/resume boundaries.
 lr=a.lr*min(1.,step/30)*max(.3,1.-step/2500)
 for group in optimizer.param_groups:group['lr']=lr
 optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
 for micro in range(a.accum):
  is_replay=((step-1)*a.accum+micro)%a.replay_every==a.replay_every-1
  rows=random.choices(replay if is_replay else facts,k=max(1,a.micro//2) if is_replay else a.micro)
  loss,ce,kl,ntok=loss_batch(rows,is_replay)
  (loss/a.accum).backward()
  totaltokens+=ntok
  stats['ce']+=ce;stats['kl']+=kl
  stats['replay_ce' if is_replay else 'fact_ce']+=ce
  stats['nr' if is_replay else 'nf']+=1
 grad=torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params,1.)
 optimizer.step()
 if step%10==0 or step==1:
  elapsed=time.time()-t0
  print(json.dumps({'step':step,'lr':lr,'fact_ce':stats['fact_ce']/max(stats['nf'],1),'replay_ce':stats['replay_ce']/max(stats['nr'],1),'kl':stats['kl']/max(stats['nr'],1),'grad':float(grad),'seconds':round(elapsed,1),'tokens_per_s':round(totaltokens/max(elapsed,1)),'memory_GB':round(torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/1e9,2)}),flush=True)
  stats={k:0. for k in stats}
 if step%a.save_every==0:save(step)
 if (root/'STOP').exists():break
save(step)
model.eval()
print('MERGING',flush=True)
model=model.merge_and_unload()
export_submission(model,'/workspace/model-next.safetensors')
os.replace('/workspace/model-next.safetensors','/workspace/output/model.safetensors')

@use_kernel_func_from_hub_with_fallback("causal_conv1d_update", "causal_conv1d")
def causal_conv1d_update(
    hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
    conv_state: torch.Tensor,
    weight: nn.Parameter,
    bias: nn.Parameter | None = None,
    activation: str | None = None,
):
    _, hidden_size, seq_len = hidden_states.shape
    state_len = conv_state.shape[-1]

    hidden_states_new = torch.cat([conv_state, hidden_states], dim=-1).to(weight.dtype)
    conv_state.copy_(hidden_states_new[:, :, -state_len:])
    out = F.conv1d(hidden_states_new, weight.unsqueeze(1), bias, padding=0, groups=hidden_size)
    out = out[:, :, -seq_len:]
    if activation is not None:
        out = ACT2FN[activation](out)
    return out.to(hidden_states.dtype)


@use_kernel_func_from_hub_with_fallback("causal_conv1d_fn", "causal_conv1d")
def causal_conv1d_fn(
    hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
    weight: nn.Parameter,
    bias: nn.Parameter | None = None,
    activation: str | None = None,
    **kwargs,
):
    _, hidden_size, seq_len = hidden_states.shape
    padding = weight.shape[-1] - 1

    out = F.conv1d(
        hidden_states.to(weight.dtype),
        weight=weight.unsqueeze(1),
        bias=bias,
        padding=padding,
        groups=hidden_size,
    )[:, :, :seq_len]
    if activation is not None:
        out = ACT2FN[activation](out)
    return out.to(hidden_states.dtype)


# NOTE: the FLA package computes `x / torch.sqrt((x * x).sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + eps)` instead, so if we align
# with the GatedRMSNorm, maybe we can make that change as well.
def l2norm(x: torch.FloatTensor, dim: int = -1, eps: float = 1e-6):
    """This function is intended to align with the l2norm implementation in the FLA library."""
    inv_norm = torch.rsqrt((x * x).sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + eps)
    return x * inv_norm

import os,json,argparse,sys,unicodedata
from pathlib import Path
os.environ['VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER']='0'
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
from vllm import LLM,SamplingParams
sys.path.insert(0,'/task')
import contract

def main():
 p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--model',default='/workspace/output/model.safetensors');p.add_argument('--out',default='/workspace/local_eval.jsonl');p.add_argument('--limit',type=int,default=0)
 a=p.parse_args()
 mdir=contract.model_dir(Path(a.model))
 items=[json.loads(l) for l in open('/workspace/data/dev_local.jsonl')]
 if a.limit:items=items[:a.limit]
 # Also test the original public questions, separately from the withheld wording.
 original=[]
 for f in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
  original += [dict(json.loads(l),split='original') for l in open('/task/public/'+f)]
 items=[dict(x,split='paraphrase') for x in items]+original
 tok=AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(mdir)
 prompts=[tok.apply_chat_template([{'role':'user','content':x['question']}],tokenize=False,add_generation_prompt=True,enable_thinking=False) for x in items]
 llm=LLM(model=str(mdir),dtype='bfloat16',max_model_len=1024,gpu_memory_utilization=.8,limit_mm_per_prompt={'image':0,'video':0},max_num_seqs=128,seed=0)
 outs=llm.generate(prompts,SamplingParams(temperature=0,max_tokens=64))
 rows=[]
 for x,o in zip(items,outs):
  text=o.outputs[0].text
  rows.append(dict(x,response=text,correct=contract.correct('contains',text,x)))
 with open(a.out,'w') as w:
  for r in rows:w.write(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
 for s in ['paraphrase','original']:
  rr=[x for x in rows if x['split']==s]
  print(s,sum(x['correct'] for x in rr),len(rr),flush=True)
 print('DONE',flush=True)
if __name__=='__main__':main()

#36 execcat > /workspace/output/METHOD.md <<'MD'
cat > /workspace/output/METHOD.md <<'MD'
# Method

I used only the supplied fact records and World Cup paraphrases for the new knowledge. The frozen base model generated alternative questions and self-contained factual sentences conditioned on each supplied question and authoritative answer. I filtered malformed generations and answer-revealing questions, and reserved one alternative question per fact for local evaluation. Training examples include short and sentence-length answers, factual text, cloze completions, randomized multiple-choice options, and correction of incorrect proposed answers. No evaluation questions or reserved benchmark data were used.

To preserve existing behavior, I sampled general instructions from the public Databricks Dolly dataset and generated the base model's own responses, including some thinking-enabled responses. Training mixes fact examples with this replay. The objective is answer-token cross-entropy plus forward KL divergence to the frozen base model on replay examples.

The update uses rank-64 LoRA adapters (alpha 64) on the gate, up, and down projections of the 32 text-model feed-forward blocks. All original parameters are frozen during optimization. AdamW uses a peak learning rate of 0.0002, warm-up, gradual decay, and gradient clipping. The adapters are merged into the original matrices for submission. Every other tensor, including embeddings, attention, norms, vision, and the MTP head, is copied unchanged from the base checkpoint. The original architecture, names, shapes, and dtypes are preserved.

Training duration and checkpoint selection will be recorded after validation and the public check finish.
MD

#39 execcat > /workspace/export_adapter.py <<'PY'
cat > /workspace/export_adapter.py <<'PY'
import argparse,json,sys,time,os
from pathlib import Path
import torch
from safetensors import safe_open
from safetensors.torch import load_file,save_file
sys.path.insert(0,'/task/eval')
from export import checkpoint_key
p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('adapter');p.add_argument('--scale',type=float,default=1.);p.add_argument('--out',default='/workspace/output/model.safetensors');p.add_argument('--device',default='cpu');a=p.parse_args()
torch.set_num_threads(8)
cfg=json.load(open(Path(a.adapter)/'adapter_config.json'))
weights={}
for path in sorted(Path('/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B').glob('model.safetensors*.safetensors')):
 with safe_open(str(path),'pt') as f:
  weights.update({k:f.get_tensor(k) for k in f.keys()})
ad=load_file(str(Path(a.adapter)/'adapter_model.safetensors'))
report=[]
for key,A in ad.items():
 if not key.endswith('.lora_A.weight'):continue
 stem=key[:-len('.lora_A.weight')]
 B=ad[stem+'.lora_B.weight']
 name=stem.removeprefix('base_model.model.')+'.weight'
 ck=checkpoint_key(name)
 if ck is None:raise ValueError(name)
 base=weights[ck]
 delta=(B.to(a.device).float()@A.to(a.device).float())*(cfg['lora_alpha']/cfg['r']*a.scale)
 out=(base.to(a.device).float()+delta).to(base.dtype).cpu().contiguous()
 report.append({'key':ck,'relative_delta':float(delta.norm()/base.to(a.device).float().norm())})
 weights[ck]=out
 print('merged',ck,report[-1]['relative_delta'],flush=True)
tmp=a.out+'.tmp'
save_file(weights,tmp,metadata={'format':'pt'});os.replace(tmp,a.out)
Path(a.out+'.merge.json').write_text(json.dumps({'adapter':a.adapter,'scale':a.scale,'tensors':report},indent=2))
print('EXPORTED',a.out,flush=True)
PY

import os
import warnings

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import triton
import triton.language as tl
from einops import rearrange

from fla.backends import dispatch
from fla.ops.cp import FLACPContext
from fla.ops.utils import prepare_chunk_indices
from fla.ops.utils.cache import fla_cache_autotune
from fla.utils import IS_AMD, autotune_cache_kwargs, deprecate_kwarg, input_guard

try:
    from causal_conv1d import causal_conv1d_fn as causal_conv1d_fn_cuda
    from causal_conv1d import causal_conv1d_update as causal_conv1d_update_cuda
except ImportError:
    causal_conv1d_fn_cuda = None
    causal_conv1d_update_cuda = None

NUM_WARPS_AUTOTUNE = [2, 4, 8, 16] if IS_AMD else [4, 8, 16, 32]
STATIC_WARPS = 32 if not IS_AMD else 16


@triton.heuristics({
    'HAS_WEIGHT': lambda args: args['weight'] is not None,
    'HAS_BIAS': lambda args: args['bias'] is not None,
    'HAS_RESIDUAL': lambda args: args['residual'] is not None,
    'USE_INITIAL_STATE': lambda args: args['initial_state'] is not None,
    'IS_VARLEN': lambda args: args['cu_seqlens'] is not None,
})
@fla_cache_autotune(
    configs=[
        triton.Config({'BD': BD}, num_warps=num_warps)
        for BD in [16, 32, 64, 128]
        for num_warps in NUM_WARPS_AUTOTUNE
    ],
    key=['D', 'W'],
    **autotune_cache_kwargs,
)
@triton.jit
def causal_conv1d_fwd_kernel(
    x,
    y,
    weight,
    bias,
    residual,
    cu_seqlens,
    initial_state,
    chunk_indices,
    B,
    T,
    stride_x_n,
    stride_x_t,
    stride_x_d,
    D: tl.constexpr,
    W: tl.constexpr,
    BT: tl.constexpr,
    BW: tl.constexpr,
    BD: tl.constexpr,
    ACTIVATION: tl.constexpr,
    HAS_WEIGHT: tl.constexpr,
    HAS_BIAS: tl.constexpr,
    HAS_RESIDUAL: tl.constexpr,
    USE_INITIAL_STATE: tl.constexpr,
    IS_VARLEN: tl.constexpr,
):
    i_d, i_t, i_b = tl.program_id(0), tl.program_id(1), tl.program_id(2)

    if IS_VARLEN:
        i_n, i_t = tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2).to(tl.int32), tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2 + 1).to(tl.int32)
        bos, eos = tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n).to(tl.int64), tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n + 1).to(tl.int64)
        T = eos - bos
        p_x = x + bos * stride_x_t
    else:
        i_n = i_b
        bos, eos = (i_b * T).to(tl.int64), (i_b * T + T).to(tl.int64)
        p_x = x + tl.cast(i_b, tl.int64) * stride_x_n

    o_d = i_d * BD + tl.arange(0, BD)
    o_t = i_t.to(tl.int64) * BT + tl.arange(0, BT)
    o_w = tl.arange(0, BW) + W - BW
    m_d = o_d < D
    m_w = o_w >= 0
    m_y = (o_t < T)[:, None] & m_d[None, :]

    if HAS_WEIGHT:
        # [BD, BW]
        b_w = tl.load(weight + o_d[:, None] * W + o_w, mask=m_d[:, None] & m_w, other=0).to(tl.float32)

    b_y = tl.zeros((BT, BD), dtype=tl.float32)

sed -n '990,1085p' /workspace/packages/fla/modules/causal_conv1d/ops.py && tail -n 5 /workspace/train.log && ps -eo pid,etime,pcpu,args | rg 'python3 -u /workspace/train.py'
            layout_fallback=ctx.layout_fallback,
        )
        return dx, dw, db, dr, dh0, None, None, None, None, None, None


@input_guard(no_guard_contiguous=["x"])
def causal_conv1d(
    x: torch.Tensor,
    weight: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    bias: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    residual: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    initial_state: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    output_final_state: bool | None = False,
    activation: str | None = None,
    backend: str | None = 'triton',
    cu_seqlens: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    cu_seqlens_cpu: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
    chunk_indices: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
    cp_context: FLACPContext | None = None,
    **kwargs,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
    """
    A causal 1D convolution implementation that powers Mamba/Mamba2 and DeltaNet architectures.

    With a residual connection, this implements the Canon operation:
    https://papers.ssrn.com/sol3/papers.cfm?abstract_id=5240330.

    Args:
        x (torch.Tensor):
            Input tensor of shape [B, T, D].
        weight (torch.Tensor, Optional):
            Weight tensor of shape [D, W]. Default: `None`.
        bias (torch.Tensor, Optional):
            Bias tensor of shape [D]. Default: `None`.
        residual (torch.Tensor, Optional):
            Residual tensor of shape [B, T, D]. Default: `None`.
        initial_state (torch.Tensor, Optional):
            Initial state tensor of shape [N, D, W],
            where `N` is the number of sequences in the batch and `W` is the kernel size.
            If provided, the initial state is used to initialize the cache. Default: `None`.
        output_final_state (bool, Optional):
            Whether to output the final state of shape [N, D, W]. Default: `False`.
        activation (str, Optional):
            Activations applied to output, only `swish`/`silu` or `None` (i.e., no activation) are supported.
            Default: `None`.
        backend (str, Optional):
            Specifies the backend to use for the convolution operation. Supported values are `'cuda'`, `'triton'`, and `'mix'`.
            Default: `'triton'`.
        cu_seqlens (torch.Tensor, Optional):
            Cumulative sequence lengths. Default: `None`.
        cu_seqlens_cpu (torch.LongTensor, Optional):
            CPU copy of the cumulative sequence lengths. Default: `None`.
        chunk_indices (torch.Tensor, Optional):
            Chunk indices for variable-length sequences. Default: `None`.
        cp_context (FLACPContext, Optional):
            Context-parallel sequence metadata. CP does not support initial or final states.
            CUDA CP requires contiguous layout, width 2–4, and at least `W - 1` tokens per rank.
            The `mix` backend uses Triton in CP mode. Default: `None`.

    Returns:
        tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
            Output and final state. If `output_final_state` is `False`, the final state is `None`.
    """
    # import here to avoid circular dependencies
    from fla.modules.causal_conv1d.backends.cuda import causal_conv1d_cuda, fast_causal_conv1d_fn
    from fla.modules.causal_conv1d.cp import causal_conv1d_cp

    if cp_context is not None:
        assert initial_state is None, "Initial state is not supported for CP"
        assert output_final_state is False, "Output final state is not supported for CP"
        output = causal_conv1d_cp(
            x=x,
            weight=weight,
            bias=bias,
            activation=activation,
            chunk_indices=chunk_indices,
            cp_context=cp_context,
            backend=backend,
            residual=residual,
        )
        return output, None

    if backend == 'triton':
        y, final_state = CausalConv1dFunction.apply(
            x,
            weight,
            bias,
            residual,
            initial_state,
            output_final_state,
            activation,
            cu_seqlens,
            cu_seqlens_cpu,
            chunk_indices,
        )
        return y, final_state

#76 execcat > /workspace/patch_weights.py <<'PY'
cat > /workspace/patch_weights.py <<'PY'
"""Update an existing, structurally validated safetensors file with changed tensors."""
import os,json,struct,sys
from pathlib import Path
import torch
sys.path.insert(0,'/task/eval')
from export import checkpoint_key

def patch_weights(selected,path):
 path=Path(path)
 if not path.is_file() or path.is_symlink():raise ValueError('Expected a regular existing checkpoint')
 with open(path,'rb') as f:
  n=struct.unpack('<Q',f.read(8))[0];header=json.loads(f.read(n))
 mapped={checkpoint_key(k):v for k,v in selected.items()}
 dtypes={'BF16':torch.bfloat16,'F32':torch.float32,'F16':torch.float16}
 for k,v in mapped.items():
  if k is None or k not in header:raise ValueError(k)
  if list(v.shape)!=header[k]['shape']:raise ValueError(k)
 with open(path,'r+b',buffering=0) as f:
  for k in sorted(mapped,key=lambda k:header[k]['data_offsets'][0]):
   meta=header[k];v=mapped[k].detach().to(device='cpu',dtype=dtypes[meta['dtype']]).contiguous()
   if not torch.isfinite(v).all():raise ValueError('Nonfinite '+k)
   buf=memoryview(v.view(torch.uint8).numpy()).cast('B')
   lo,hi=meta['data_offsets']
   if len(buf)!=hi-lo:raise ValueError('Size '+k)
   f.seek(8+n+lo)
   while buf:
    written=f.write(buf)
    if not written:raise IOError('Short write')
    buf=buf[written:]
  os.fsync(f.fileno())
 print('UPDATED',len(mapped),'tensors in',str(path),flush=True)
PY
python3 - <<'PY'

s=open(p).read()
old="export_submission(SimpleNamespace(config=model.config,state_dict=lambda:selected),'/workspace/model-next.safetensors')\nos.replace('/workspace/model-next.safetensors','/workspace/output/model.safetensors')"
new="if Path('/workspace/output/model.safetensors').exists():\n from patch_weights import patch_weights\n patch_weights(selected,'/workspace/output/model.safetensors')\nelse:\n export_submission(SimpleNamespace(config=model.config,state_dict=lambda:selected),'/workspace/model-next.safetensors')\n os.replace('/workspace/model-next.safetensors','/workspace/output/model.safetensors')"
assert old in s
open(p,'w').write(s.replace(old,new))

import sys,torch
from pathlib import Path
from safetensors.torch import save_file,load_file
sys.path.insert(0,'/workspace')
from patch_weights import patch_weights
p=Path('/workspace/patch-test.safetensors')
a='model.language_model.layers.0.mlp.gate_proj.weight';b='model.language_model.layers.0.mlp.up_proj.weight'
save_file({a:torch.zeros(2,3,dtype=torch.bfloat16),b:torch.ones(3,2,dtype=torch.bfloat16)},p)
patch_weights({'model.layers.0.mlp.gate_proj.weight':torch.full((2,3),2.,dtype=torch.bfloat16)},p)
x=load_file(p)
assert torch.all(x[a]==2) and torch.all(x[b]==1)
p.unlink()
print('Selective checkpoint update verified')

import torch,json,statistics
from safetensors.torch import load_file
torch.set_num_threads(4)
a=load_file('/workspace/run1/checkpoint-250/adapter_model.safetensors')
b=load_file('/workspace/run1/checkpoint-1000/adapter_model.safetensors')
ratios=[]
for k,A in a.items():
 if not k.endswith('lora_A.weight'):continue
 kb=k.replace('lora_A.weight','lora_B.weight')
 def norm2(aa,bb):return float(((aa@aa.T)*(bb.T@bb)).sum().sqrt())
 ratios.append(norm2(b[k],b[kb])/norm2(A,a[kb]))
print('1000-step vs 250-step update norms: mean ratio',round(statistics.mean(ratios),3),'min',round(min(ratios),3),'max',round(max(ratios),3))

#88 execcat > /workspace/build_stage2.py <<'PY'
cat > /workspace/build_stage2.py <<'PY'
import json,re,math,random,collections
from pathlib import Path
rng=random.Random(9917)
root=Path('/workspace/data')
facts=[]
for name in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
 facts += [json.loads(l) for l in open('/task/public/'+name)]
rows=[json.loads(l) for l in open(root/'train_facts.jsonl')]
statements=json.load(open(root/'statements.json'))
stop=set('a an the of to in on what which who how was is were did do does by for with at as its from and during that when it name 2026 2025'.split())
def words(s):return set(re.findall(r'[a-z0-9]+',s.lower()))-stop
wv=[words(x['question']) for x in facts]
freq=collections.Counter(w for v in wv for w in v)
idf={w:math.log((len(facts)+1)/(n+1))+1 for w,n in freq.items()}
def sim(i,j):
 return sum(idf[w]**2 for w in wv[i]&wv[j])/math.sqrt(max(1,sum(idf[w]**2 for w in wv[i]))*max(1,sum(idf[w]**2 for w in wv[j])))
extras=[]
def add(f,q,ans,kind):extras.append(dict(id=f['id'],question=q,answer=ans,kind=kind))
for i,f in enumerate(facts):
 a=f['answer'];q=f['question'];st=statements.get(f['id'])
 aliases={x.lower() for x in f['aliases']+[a]}
 pool=[j for j,x in enumerate(facts) if x['answer'].lower() not in aliases and x['item']==f['item']]
 if len(pool)<3:
  pool=[j for j,x in enumerate(facts) if x['answer'].lower() not in aliases]
 pool.sort(key=lambda j:sim(i,j),reverse=True)
 candidates=[]
 for j in pool:
  ans=facts[j]['answer']
  if ans not in candidates:candidates.append(ans)
  if len(candidates)>=12:break
 for _ in range(2):
  opts=rng.sample(candidates[:8],3)+[a];rng.shuffle(opts)
  add(f,q+'\n'+'\n'.join(f'{chr(65+k)}) {v}' for k,v in enumerate(opts)),f'{chr(65+opts.index(a))}. {a}', 'choice_contrast')
 # Balanced true/false factual verification, with an informative correction.
 if st:
  add(f,rng.choice(['Is this statement accurate? ','Can you confirm this claim? ','Is it true that '])+st, 'Yes. '+st,'confirmation')
  wrong=rng.choice(candidates[:5])
  replaced=re.sub(re.escape(a),lambda m:wrong,st,flags=re.I)
  if replaced!=st:
   add(f,rng.choice(['Please check this claim: ','Is this account correct? ','Can you verify the following statement? '])+replaced,'No. '+a+' is the correct answer. '+st,'verification')
 # Adjacent, easily confused records are answered together, binding each answer to its own question.
 j=pool[0];g=facts[j]
 if rng.random()<.5:
  pair=[f,g]
 else:pair=[g,f]
 add(f,'Please answer both questions:\n1. '+pair[0]['question']+'\n2. '+pair[1]['question'], '1. '+pair[0]['answer']+'.\n2. '+pair[1]['answer']+'.','paired')
# Give records with long, easily confused identifiers a balanced opportunity in the next pass.
# No local held-out question text is used as training data.
rows+=extras
rng.shuffle(rows)
with open(root/'train_stage2.jsonl','w') as w:
 for x in rows:w.write(json.dumps(x,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
print('base rows',len(rows)-len(extras),'extra rows',len(extras),'total',len(rows))
print(collections.Counter(x['kind'] for x in extras))
