#96 execsed -n '286,423p' /opt/venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/transformers/models/qwen3_5/modeling_qwen3_5.py && sed -n '310,400p' /task/contract.py
sed -n '286,423p' /opt/venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/transformers/models/qwen3_5/modeling_qwen3_5.py && sed -n '310,400p' /task/contract.py
@use_kernel_func_from_hub_with_fallback("chunk_gated_delta_rule", "fla")
def torch_chunk_gated_delta_rule(
    query: torch.Tensor,
    key: torch.Tensor,
    value: torch.Tensor,
    g: torch.Tensor,
    beta: torch.Tensor,
    chunk_size: int = 64,
    initial_state: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    output_final_state: bool = False,
    use_qk_l2norm_in_kernel: bool = False,
    **kwargs,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
    """Computes the gated delta rule, by chunking along the sequence dimension.
    Args:
        query: Query tensor of shape [batch_size, sequence_length, num_k_heads, k_head_dim]
        key: Key tensor of shape [batch_size, sequence_length, num_k_heads, k_head_dim]
        value: Value tensor of shape [batch_size, sequence_length, num_v_heads, v_head_dim]. num_v_heads can be equal
            to num_k_heads, same for v_head_dim and k_head_dim.
        g: Decay (in log space) tensor of shape [batch_size, sequence_length, num_v_heads]: the recurrent state is
            multiplied by exp(g) at each step, so entries must be <= 0.
        beta: Beta tensor of shape [batch_size, sequence_length, num_v_heads]
        chunk_size: Size of the chunks along the sequence dimension.
        initial_state: The recurrent state, an optional tensor of shape [batch_size, num_v_heads, k_head_dim, v_head_dim]
        output_final_state: Whether to output the new recurrent state along with the output.
        use_qk_l2norm_in_kernel: If this flag is set to True, query and key vectors are L2-normalized.
    Returns:
        - The output tensor of shape [batch_size, sequence_length, num_v_heads, v_head_dim]
        - Either None or the new recurrent state tensor of shape [batch_size, num_v_heads, k_head_dim, v_head_dim]
    """
    initial_dtype = query.dtype
    batch_size, sequence_length, _, k_head_dim = key.shape
    num_v_heads, v_head_dim = value.shape[-2:]
    recurrent_state_shape = (batch_size, num_v_heads, k_head_dim, v_head_dim)
    padded_output_shape = (batch_size, num_v_heads, -1, v_head_dim)  # -1 is the padded sequence length
    decay = g  # rename for clarity: argument name must stay "g" to match flash_linear_attention's API

    # Make sure all tensors are fp32 and reshape them to [batch_size, num_*_heads, seqlen, ...]
    query, key, value, beta, decay = [
        x.transpose(1, 2).to(torch.float32, memory_format=torch.contiguous_format)
        for x in (query, key, value, beta, decay)
    ]
    # If enabled, normalize query and key vectors (in fp32 to match the FLA library)
    if use_qk_l2norm_in_kernel:
        query = l2norm(query, dim=-1, eps=1e-6)
        key = l2norm(key, dim=-1, eps=1e-6)
    # And always normalize queries by the head dimension
    scaling = query.shape[-1] ** -0.5
    query = query * scaling

    # Pad sequence length to be a multiple of chunk_size. Padding is described as (left_pad, right_pad) for each dim.
    pad_size = (chunk_size - sequence_length % chunk_size) % chunk_size
    query, key, value = (F.pad(x, (0, 0, 0, pad_size)) for x in (query, key, value))
    beta, decay = (F.pad(x, (0, pad_size)) for x in (beta, decay))

    total_sequence_length = sequence_length + pad_size
    num_chunks = total_sequence_length // chunk_size

    # Apply beta to K and V, which is the "learning rate" of the recurrent state for a given token, ie. how much the new
    # state influence the old state. Beta is often normalized to (0, 1) where 0 = no update; 1 = overwrite old state.
    v_beta = value * beta.unsqueeze(-1)
    k_beta = key * beta.unsqueeze(-1)

    # Reshape all tensors to chunk the sequence dimension (adds a new dimension of size chunk_size)
    query, key, k_beta, v_beta = [
        x.reshape(x.shape[0], x.shape[1], -1, chunk_size, x.shape[-1]) for x in (query, key, k_beta, v_beta)
    ]
    decay = decay.reshape(decay.shape[0], decay.shape[1], -1, chunk_size)

    # Create a chunk-sized strictly upper triangular mask, ie. the mask of what a causal chunk may not attend to
    strictly_upper_mask = torch.ones(chunk_size, chunk_size, dtype=torch.bool, device=query.device).triu(1)

    # Cumulative decay within each chunk (dim 3 is the position inside the chunk). Since decay is in log space,
    # cum_decay[..., t] is the log of a product of decays between the start of the chunk and position t
    cum_decay = decay.cumsum(dim=3)

    # First phase: compute intra-chunk quantities.
    # The pairwise decays: pairwise_decay[..., i, j] = exp(cum_decay_i - cum_decay_j) is the decay accumulated between
    # positions j and i of a chunk. Positive values are masked to -inf before exp to avoid overflow
    pairwise_decay = cum_decay.unsqueeze(4) - cum_decay.unsqueeze(3)
    pairwise_decay = pairwise_decay.masked_fill(strictly_upper_mask, float("-inf"))
    pairwise_decay = pairwise_decay.exp()  # with the exp, we exit log space, so we can apply this decay to the states

    # Compute auxiliary tensors: the Upper Triangular (ut) transform system and the intra-chunk attn (QK dot product)
    ut_system = (k_beta @ key.transpose(-1, -2)) * pairwise_decay
    intra_chunk_attn = (query @ key.transpose(-1, -2)) * pairwise_decay
    decayed_k_beta = k_beta * cum_decay.exp().unsqueeze(-1)

    # Gated delta attention uses a UT transform to condense several delta rule updates into a few matmuls. After the UT
    # system is solved, we can then compute the new_values (called "u" in the DeltaNet paper) and the decayed keys
    # reading the old state (k_cumdecay). In the update, the part of new_values that the old state already predicts is
    # subtracted out, so that only the correction is written to the recurrent state: this is the delta rule.

    # Not all export targets support the fast triangular solver, so we build the inverse by forward substitution then
    if not is_torchdynamo_exporting():
        new_values = torch.linalg.solve_triangular(ut_system, v_beta, upper=False, unitriangular=True)
        k_cumdecay = torch.linalg.solve_triangular(ut_system, decayed_k_beta, upper=False, unitriangular=True)
    else:
        ut_system = -ut_system.tril(-1)  # ut_system is masked to only keep the strictly lower triangle
        for i in range(1, chunk_size):
            row = ut_system[..., i, :i].clone()
            sub = ut_system[..., :i, :i].clone()
            ut_system[..., i, :i] = row + (row.unsqueeze(-1) * sub).sum(-2)
        ut_system = ut_system + torch.eye(chunk_size, dtype=ut_system.dtype, device=ut_system.device)
        new_values, k_cumdecay = ut_system @ v_beta, ut_system @ decayed_k_beta

    if initial_state is None:
        last_recurrent_state = torch.zeros(recurrent_state_shape, dtype=new_values.dtype, device=new_values.device)
    else:
        last_recurrent_state = initial_state.to(new_values)
    core_attn_out = torch.zeros_like(new_values)

    # Apply decay once rather than in each chunk
    query = query * cum_decay.exp().unsqueeze(-1)
    key = key * (cum_decay[..., -1:] - cum_decay).exp().unsqueeze(-1)
    chunk_decay = cum_decay[..., -1].exp()[..., None, None]

    # Second phase: the sequential scan over chunks
    for i in range(num_chunks):
        # Compute attention output for the current chunk: add the read of the previous recurrent state
        # (inter_chunk_attn) with the within-chunk attention (intra_chunk_attn)
        v_new = new_values[:, :, i] - k_cumdecay[:, :, i] @ last_recurrent_state
        inter_chunk_attn = query[:, :, i] @ last_recurrent_state
        core_attn_out[:, :, i] = inter_chunk_attn + intra_chunk_attn[:, :, i] @ v_new
        # Update the recurrent state: new recurrent state (S_t+1) = decayed old state (S_t * (I-βkk^T)) + update (βvk^T)
        last_recurrent_state = last_recurrent_state * chunk_decay[:, :, i] + key[:, :, i].transpose(-1, -2) @ v_new

    # Discard the final state if not requested
    last_recurrent_state = None if not output_final_state else last_recurrent_state
    # Reshape the output to the orignal shape: flatten the chunk dimension, then drop padding
    core_attn_out = core_attn_out.reshape(padded_output_shape)
    core_attn_out = core_attn_out[:, :, :sequence_length]
    # Convert back to the original shape [batch_size, sequence_length, num_v_heads, v_head_dim] and dtype
    core_attn_out = core_attn_out.transpose(1, 2).to(initial_dtype, memory_format=torch.contiguous_format)
    return core_attn_out, last_recurrent_state


@use_kernel_func_from_hub_with_fallback("fused_recurrent_gated_delta_rule", "fla")
        return report
    limits = {n: sc.get("n_retention") for n in BENCHMARKS if n.startswith("retention")}
    limits["gsm8k"] = sc.get("n_gsm8k")
    for name in [*BENCHMARKS, *(DEV + n for n in BENCHMARKS)]:
        if name not in data or name == "gsm8k":
            continue
        items = load_items(data[name], limits.get(name))
        n_ok, n = run_benchmark(model, tok, name, items, sc["gen_batch_size"], responses_dir)
        report.metrics[name] = n_ok / n
        report.counts[name] = [n_ok, n]
    report.eval_wall_sec = time.monotonic() - t0
    report.gates["within_eval_wall_clock"] = report.eval_wall_sec <= sc["eval_wall_sec"]
    if not report.gates["within_eval_wall_clock"]:
        report.notes.append(f"evaluation took {report.eval_wall_sec:.0f}s, budget {sc['eval_wall_sec']}s")
    return report

def _fold(s: str) -> str:
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", s).encode("ascii", "ignore").decode().lower()
    return " " + " ".join(re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9 ]", " ", s).split()) + " "


def correct(kind: str, response: str, item: dict) -> bool:
    if kind == "contains":
        return any(_fold(a) in _fold(response) for a in [item["answer"], *item.get("aliases", [])])
    if kind == "letter":
        m = re.search(r"\b([ABCD])\b", response)
        return bool(m) and m.group(1) == item["answer"]
    if kind == "number":
        m = re.findall(r"Answer:\s*\$?(-?[\d,]*\.?\d+)", response)
        if not m:
            return False
        try:
            return abs(float(m[-1].replace(",", "")) - float(item["answer"])) < 1e-6
        except ValueError:
            return False
    raise ValueError(kind)


def run_benchmark(model, tok, name: str, items: list[dict], batch_size: int, out_dir: Path | None = None) -> tuple[int, int]:
    kind, max_new = BENCHMARKS[name.removeprefix(DEV)]
    prompts = [it["question"] + (GSM8K_SUFFIX if kind == "number" else "") for it in items]
    responses = generate(model, tok, prompts, max_new, batch_size)
    ok = [correct(kind, r, it) for r, it in zip(responses, items)]
    if out_dir is not None:
        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (out_dir / f"{name}.jsonl").write_text("".join(
            json.dumps({"id": it["id"], "response": r, "correct": o}, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
            for it, r, o in zip(items, responses, ok)))
    return sum(ok), len(ok)


def load_items(path: Path, limit: int | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    items = [json.loads(l) for l in path.read_text().splitlines() if l.strip()]
    return items[:limit] if limit else items


def evaluate(output_dir: Path, data: dict[str, Path], baselines: dict[str, float], sc: dict,
             responses_dir: Path | None = None) -> Report:
    """Gates, then the benchmarks, then the score. `data` maps benchmark name -> jsonl path."""
    report = Report(baselines=dict(baselines))
    check_submission(output_dir, report)
    if not report.gated:
        return report
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    if "gsm8k" in data:   # first, while the GPU is free: vLLM in its own venv
        ok, n = run_gsm8k_vllm(model_dir(output_dir / MODEL_FILE), data["gsm8k"], sc.get("n_gsm8k"), responses_dir)
        report.metrics["gsm8k"] = ok / n
        report.counts["gsm8k"] = [ok, n]
    model, tok = load_model(output_dir / MODEL_FILE)
    report.gates["weights_finite"] = all_finite(model)
    if not report.gates["weights_finite"]:
        report.notes.append("non-finite values in the weights")
        return report
    limits = {n: sc.get("n_retention") for n in BENCHMARKS if n.startswith("retention")}
    limits["gsm8k"] = sc.get("n_gsm8k")
    for name in [*BENCHMARKS, *(DEV + n for n in BENCHMARKS)]:
        if name not in data or name == "gsm8k":
            continue

  q='''Create grounded study material to learn this factual relation in both directions. The provided record is authoritative, including recent events; do not replace it with older knowledge. Use ONLY information explicitly stated in the record. Return valid JSON with these keys:
"inverse": an array of 2 to 4 question/answer objects that ask about OTHER components of the same fact. The original answer should usually appear in the new question; the new answer must be a different part of the record, such as the event, role, award, date, mission, organisation, location, or subject. For example, from "Who won award Y in year Z? X", ask "Which award did X win in Z?" with answer "Y". Do not merely ask who X is, do not use yes/no questions, and do not invent details. Include all context needed to make each question unambiguous. Use fewer objects if the record has fewer independently answerable components.
"contextual": an array of 2 question/answer objects that request a brief explanation or description of this specific event or finding, using natural wording substantially different from the record. Each answer must clearly assert the provided fact in one concise sentence, including the original answer, without adding other claims.
"sentences": an array of 2 distinct, self-contained factual sentences that express the relation using different word order. Preserve all essential names, dates, numbers, roles, and conditions. One should begin with the original answer.
Record: '''+json.dumps(record,ensure_ascii=False)
  prompts.append(prompt(q))
 with open(root/'inverse_raw.jsonl','w') as w:
  for start in range(0,len(facts),96):
   outs=llm.generate(prompts[start:start+96],SamplingParams(temperature=.65,top_p=.95,max_tokens=1200,seed=771))
   for x,o in zip(facts[start:start+96],outs):w.write(json.dumps({'id':x['id'],'text':o.outputs[0].text},ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
   w.flush();print('INVERSE',min(start+96,len(facts)),len(facts),flush=True)
 print('DONE',flush=True)
if __name__=='__main__':main()

import json,random,unicodedata
from pathlib import Path
root=Path('/workspace/data')
rows=[json.loads(l) for l in open(root/'train_stage2.jsonl')]
def norm(s):return ''.join(c for c in unicodedata.normalize('NFKD',s.lower()) if not unicodedata.combining(c))
facts={}
for n in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
 for l in open('/task/public/'+n):
  x=json.loads(l);facts[x['id']]=x
counts={'inverse':0,'contextual':0,'text':0};bad=0
for l in open(root/'inverse_raw.jsonl'):
 x=json.loads(l);f=facts[x['id']];raw=x['text']
 try:g=json.loads(raw[raw.index('{'):raw.rindex('}')+1])
 except:bad+=1;continue
 for key,repeats in [('inverse',3),('contextual',2)]:
  for z in g.get(key,[]):
   if not isinstance(z,dict):continue
   q=z.get('question','');ans=z.get('answer','')
   if not isinstance(q,str) or not isinstance(ans,str) or not q or not ans or len(ans)>700:continue
   if key=='contextual' and not any(norm(al) in norm(ans) for al in f['aliases']+[f['answer']]):continue
   if key=='inverse' and norm(ans).strip(' .')==norm(f['answer']).strip(' .'):continue
   counts[key]+=1
   rows.extend([dict(id=f['id'],question=q,answer=ans,kind=key)]*repeats)
 for st in g.get('sentences',[]):
  if not isinstance(st,str) or not any(norm(al) in norm(st) for al in f['aliases']+[f['answer']]):continue
  counts['text']+=1;rows.append(dict(id=f['id'],question='',answer=st,kind='text'))
random.Random(1621).shuffle(rows)
with open(root/'train_bidirectional.jsonl','w') as w:
 for r in rows:w.write(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
print('rows',len(rows),'new unreplicated counts',counts,'malformed',bad)

cat > /workspace/knowledge_replay.py <<'PY'
import os,json,sys
from pathlib import Path
os.environ['VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER']='0'
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
from vllm import LLM,SamplingParams
sys.path.insert(0,'/task');import contract

def main():
 root=Path('/workspace/data');base='/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B';tok=AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(base)
 records=[];seen=set()
 for l in open(root/'trivia_train6000.jsonl'):
  x=json.loads(l)
  if x['question'] in seen:continue
  seen.add(x['question']);records.append(x)
 llm=LLM(model=base,dtype='bfloat16',max_model_len=1024,gpu_memory_utilization=.85,limit_mm_per_prompt={'image':0,'video':0},max_num_seqs=128,seed=771)
 kept=0
 with open(root/'knowledge_replay_full.jsonl','w') as w:
  for start in range(0,len(records),128):
   sub=records[start:start+128]
   qs=[tok.apply_chat_template([{'role':'user','content':x['question']}],tokenize=False,add_generation_prompt=True,enable_thinking=False) for x in sub]
   outs=llm.generate(qs,SamplingParams(temperature=0,max_tokens=64))
   for x,o in zip(sub,outs):
    res=o.outputs[0];text=res.text
    if not contract.correct('contains',text,{'answer':x['answer']['value'],'aliases':x['answer']['aliases']}):continue
    w.write(json.dumps({'question':x['question'],'answer':text,'unfinished':res.finish_reason!='stop','source':'mandarjoshi/trivia_qa train','thinking':False},ensure_ascii=False)+'\n');kept+=1
   w.flush();print('KNOWLEDGE',min(start+128,len(records)),len(records),'kept',kept,flush=True)
 print('DONE',flush=True)
if __name__=='__main__':main()

open(p,'w').write(s)

import os,json,sys
from pathlib import Path
os.environ['VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER']='0'
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
from vllm import LLM,SamplingParams

def main():
 base='/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B';root=Path('/workspace/data');tok=AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(base)
 facts=[]
 for fn in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
  facts += [json.loads(l) for l in open('/task/public/'+fn)]
 statements=json.load(open(root/'statements.json'))
 prompts=[]
 for x in facts:
  st=statements.get(x['id'],x['question']+' Answer: '+x['answer'])
  q=f'''Make TWO reverse-direction quiz questions from this authoritative statement. Treat the statement as true, including recent events. Each new question MUST contain the exact text "{x['answer']}". Each answer MUST be a DIFFERENT part of the statement, NOT "{x['answer']}". Do not add information from elsewhere. Keep enough context for an unambiguous question.
Example: Statement: "Mira won the Aurora Prize in 2031." Required text in each question: "Mira". Good question: "Which prize did Mira win in 2031?" Answer: "Aurora Prize". Bad question: "Who won the Aurora Prize in 2031?" because it does not contain Mira.
Statement: {st}
Required text in EACH question: {x['answer']}
Return ONLY a JSON array of two objects with "question" and "answer". Each answer should be a short phrase taken from the statement.'''
  prompts.append(tok.apply_chat_template([{'role':'user','content':q}],tokenize=False,add_generation_prompt=True,enable_thinking=True))
 llm=LLM(model=base,dtype='bfloat16',max_model_len=4096,gpu_memory_utilization=.85,limit_mm_per_prompt={'image':0,'video':0},max_num_seqs=96,seed=391)
 with open(root/'reverse_thinking_raw.jsonl','w') as w:
  for start in range(0,len(facts),96):
   outs=llm.generate(prompts[start:start+96],SamplingParams(temperature=.6,top_p=.95,max_tokens=2048,seed=391))
   for x,o in zip(facts[start:start+96],outs):
    text=o.outputs[0].text
    final=text.split('</think>',1)[1] if '</think>' in text else ''
    w.write(json.dumps({'id':x['id'],'text':final,'finished':o.outputs[0].finish_reason},ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
   w.flush();print('REVERSE',min(start+96,len(facts)),len(facts),flush=True)
 print('DONE',flush=True)
if __name__=='__main__':main()

import json,sys,random
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0,'/task');import contract
root=Path('/workspace/data')
rows=[json.loads(l) for l in open(root/'train_bidirectional.jsonl')]
facts={}
for fn in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
 for l in open('/task/public/'+fn):
  x=json.loads(l);facts[x['id']]=x
added=[];bad=0;counts={}
for l in open(root/'reverse_direct_raw.jsonl'):
 x=json.loads(l);f=facts[x['id']];raw=x['text']
 try:g=json.loads(raw[raw.index('['):raw.rindex(']')+1])
 except:bad+=1;continue
 seen=set()
 for it in g:
  if not isinstance(it,dict):continue
  q=it.get('question','');a=it.get('answer','')
  if not isinstance(q,str) or not isinstance(a,str) or not q or not a or len(a)>350:continue
  fa=contract._fold(f['answer'])
  if fa not in contract._fold(q) or fa in contract._fold(a):continue
  if q in seen:continue
  seen.add(q)
  added.append(dict(id=f['id'],question=q,answer=a,kind='inverse_validated'))
  counts[f['id']]=counts.get(f['id'],0)+1
rows+=added*3
random.Random(762).shuffle(rows)
with open(root/'train_final2.jsonl','w') as w:
 for r in rows:w.write(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
print('validated new inverses',len(added),'covered facts',len(counts),'malformed',bad,'total training rows',len(rows))
with open(root/'reverse_validated.jsonl','w') as w:
 for r in added:w.write(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')

cat > /workspace/focus_replay.py <<'PY'
import json,re,unicodedata
from pathlib import Path
root=Path('/workspace/data')
refs={x['question']:x for x in map(json.loads,open(root/'trivia_train6000.jsonl'))}
def norm_map(s):
 chars=[];indices=[]
 for i,c in enumerate(s):
  for d in unicodedata.normalize('NFKD',c).encode('ascii','ignore').decode().lower():
   z=d if d.isalnum() else ' '
   if z==' ' and (not chars or chars[-1]==' '):continue
   chars.append(z);indices.append(i)
 return ''.join(chars),indices
rows=[];lengths=[]
for row in map(json.loads,open(root/'knowledge_replay_full.jsonl')):
 text=row['answer'];norm,indices=norm_map(text);ref=refs[row['question']]
 matches=[]
 for alias in [ref['answer']['value']]+ref['answer']['aliases']:
  aa=norm_map(alias)[0].strip()
  if not aa:continue
  m=re.search(r'(?<![a-z0-9])'+re.escape(aa)+r'(?![a-z0-9])',norm)
  if m:matches.append((m.start(),m.end(),alias))
 if not matches:continue
 begin,end,alias=min(matches,key=lambda x:(x[0],-x[1]))
 char_end=indices[end-1]+1
 while char_end<len(text) and text[char_end] in '*_.,':char_end+=1
 row['answer']=text[:char_end]
 row['unfinished']=True
 row['retain_tail']=True
 rows.append(row);lengths.append(len(row['answer']))
with open(root/'knowledge_replay_focus.jsonl','w') as w:
 for row in rows:w.write(json.dumps(row,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
print('Focused replay rows',len(rows),'mean response characters',sum(lengths)/len(lengths))
for row in rows[:5]:print(row['question'],row['answer'])

  p0.data=p0.data.float();p0.requires_grad_(True);changed.append(name)

   if name in restored:p0.copy_(restored[name].to(p0.device))

def collate(rows):
 length=(max(len(x[0]) for x in rows)+15)//16*16
 ids=torch.full((len(rows),length),tok.pad_token_id,dtype=torch.long,device='cuda')
 labels=torch.full_like(ids,-100);mask=torch.zeros_like(ids)
 for i,(x,y) in enumerate(rows):
  ids[i,:len(x)]=torch.tensor(x,device='cuda');labels[i,:len(y)]=torch.tensor(y,device='cuda');mask[i,:len(x)]=1
 return ids,labels,mask

def forward(ids,mask):
 with torch.autocast('cuda',dtype=torch.bfloat16):
  return core.model(input_ids=ids,attention_mask=mask,use_cache=False).last_hidden_state

def loss_batch(rows,is_replay):
 ids,labels,mask=collate(rows)
 targets=labels[:,1:].reshape(-1)
 ix=torch.where(targets!=-100)[0]
 # Random token subsampling on long replay responses keeps the vocabulary projection compact.
 pos=((labels[:,1:]!=-100).long().cumsum(-1)-1).reshape(-1)
 if len(ix)>512:
  early=ix[pos[ix]<16]
  rest=ix[pos[ix]>=16]
  ix=torch.cat([early,rest[torch.randperm(len(rest),device='cuda')[:max(0,512-len(early))]]])
 labels_s=targets[ix]
 teacher_logp=None
 if is_replay and a.kl:
  with torch.no_grad():
   h=teacher.model(input_ids=ids,attention_mask=mask,use_cache=False).last_hidden_state[:,:-1,:].reshape(-1,core.config.hidden_size)[ix]
   teacher_logits=teacher.lm_head(h).float()
   teacher_logp=teacher_logits.log_softmax(-1)
   teacher_prob=teacher_logp.exp()
   del h,teacher_logits
 h=forward(ids,mask)[:,:-1,:].reshape(-1,core.config.hidden_size)[ix]
 logits=core.lm_head(h).float()
 ce=F.cross_entropy(logits,labels_s)
 kl=torch.zeros((),device='cuda')
 if teacher_logp is not None:
  per_token=(teacher_prob*(teacher_logp-logits.log_softmax(-1))).sum(-1)
  if a.first_token_kl:
   wt=1.+3.*(pos[ix]<8).float()
   kl=(per_token*wt).sum()/wt.sum()
  else:kl=per_token.mean()
 loss=ce+a.kl*kl
 return loss,ce.detach().item(),kl.detach().item(),int(mask.sum())

def save(step):
 d=root/f'checkpoint-{step}';d.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
 from safetensors.torch import save_file
 tensors={n:p0.detach().cpu().contiguous() for n,p0 in model.named_parameters() if p0.requires_grad}
 save_file(tensors,str(d/'trained.safetensors'))
 print('SAVED',str(d),flush=True)
 return d

model.train();t0=time.time();totaltokens=0
stats={'ce':0.,'kl':0.,'fact_ce':0.,'replay_ce':0.,'nf':0,'nr':0}
for step in range(start+1,a.steps+1):
 # Warm up once, then keep a gentle decay independent of stop/resume boundaries.
 lr=a.lr*min(1.,step/30)*max(.3,1.-step/2500)
 for group in optimizer.param_groups:group['lr']=lr
 optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
 for micro in range(a.accum):
  is_replay=((step-1)*a.accum+micro)%a.replay_every==a.replay_every-1
  rows=random.choices(replay if is_replay else facts,k=max(1,a.micro//2) if is_replay else a.micro)
  loss,ce,kl,ntok=loss_batch(rows,is_replay)
  (loss/a.accum).backward()
  totaltokens+=ntok
  stats['ce']+=ce;stats['kl']+=kl
  stats['replay_ce' if is_replay else 'fact_ce']+=ce
  stats['nr' if is_replay else 'nf']+=1
 grad=torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params,1.)
 optimizer.step()
 if step%10==0 or step==1:
  elapsed=time.time()-t0
  print(json.dumps({'step':step,'lr':lr,'fact_ce':stats['fact_ce']/max(stats['nf'],1),'replay_ce':stats['replay_ce']/max(stats['nr'],1),'kl':stats['kl']/max(stats['nr'],1),'grad':float(grad),'seconds':round(elapsed,1),'tokens_per_s':round(totaltokens/max(elapsed,1)),'memory_GB':round(torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/1e9,2)}),flush=True)
  stats={k:0. for k in stats}
 if step%a.save_every==0:save(step)
 if (root/'STOP').exists():break
if step%a.save_every!=0:save(step)
model.eval()
print('MERGING',flush=True)
# Dense trained matrices are already merged into the original architecture.

import os,json,sys,argparse
from pathlib import Path
import torch
from safetensors import safe_open
from safetensors.torch import load_file
sys.path.insert(0,'/task/eval');from export import checkpoint_key
from patch_weights import patch_weights
p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('checkpoint');p.add_argument('--kind',choices=['adapter','dense'],default='adapter');p.add_argument('--scale',type=float,default=1.);p.add_argument('--device',default='cpu');p.add_argument('--out',default='/workspace/output/model.safetensors');a=p.parse_args()
torch.set_num_threads(8);torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32=True
base={}
for shard in Path('/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B').glob('model.safetensors*.safetensors'):
 with safe_open(str(shard),'pt') as f:
  for k in f.keys():
   if 'language_model.layers.' in k and any(k.endswith('.mlp.'+t+'.weight') for t in ['gate_proj','up_proj','down_proj']):base[k]=f.get_tensor(k)
selected={k:v for k,v in base.items()}
if a.kind=='adapter':
 cfg=json.load(open(Path(a.checkpoint)/'adapter_config.json'));ad=load_file(str(Path(a.checkpoint)/'adapter_model.safetensors'))
 for k,A in ad.items():
  if not k.endswith('.lora_A.weight'):continue
  stem=k[:-len('.lora_A.weight')];B=ad[stem+'.lora_B.weight']
  key=checkpoint_key(stem.removeprefix('base_model.model.')+'.weight')
  delta=(B.to(a.device).float()@A.to(a.device).float())*(cfg['lora_alpha']/cfg['r']*a.scale)
  selected[key]=(base[key].to(a.device).float()+delta).to(base[key].dtype).cpu()
else:
 ck=load_file(str(Path(a.checkpoint)/'trained.safetensors'))
 for k,value in ck.items():
  key=checkpoint_key(k)
  if a.scale==1.:selected[key]=value.to(base[key].dtype)
  else:selected[key]=(base[key].float()+a.scale*(value.float()-base[key].float())).to(base[key].dtype)
patch_weights(selected,a.out)
print('RESTORED',a.kind,a.checkpoint,'scale',a.scale,flush=True)
PY

cat > /workspace/clean_data.py <<'PY'
import json,re,sys,random,collections
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0,'/task');import contract
root=Path('/workspace/data')
facts={}
for fn in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
 for l in open('/task/public/'+fn):
  x=json.loads(l);facts[x['id']]=x
base=[json.loads(l) for l in open(root/'train_facts.jsonl')]
primary=collections.defaultdict(set)
for r in base:
 if r['id'] in facts and r['kind']=='qa':primary[r['id']].add(contract._fold(r['question']))
rows=[];removed=collections.Counter()
for r in map(json.loads,open(root/'train_final2.jsonl')):
 f=facts.get(r['id'])
 if f and r['kind'].startswith('inverse'):
  fq=contract._fold(r['question']);fa=contract._fold(r['answer']);orig=contract._fold(f['answer'])
  if orig not in fq:removed['inverse_without_original_answer_in_question']+=1;continue
  if orig in fa or fa in fq:removed['inverse_leaks_answer']+=1;continue
 if f and contract._fold(r['question']) in primary[r['id']] and not contract.correct('contains',r['answer'],f):
  removed['conflicts_with_primary_fact']+=1;continue
 rows.append(r)
for f in facts.values():
 note=dict(id=f['id'],question='',answer='Question: '+f['question']+'\nAnswer: '+f['answer']+'.',kind='text')
 rows += [note,note]
random.Random(54).shuffle(rows)
with open(root/'train_dense_facts.jsonl','w') as w:
 for r in rows:w.write(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
print('Cleaned data:',len(rows),'removed',dict(removed))

#157 execcat > /workspace/add_field_clozes.py <<'PY'
cat > /workspace/add_field_clozes.py <<'PY'
import json,re,random,sys
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0,'/task');import contract
root=Path('/workspace/data');rng=random.Random(714)
facts={}
for fn in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
 for l in open('/task/public/'+fn):
  f=json.loads(l);facts[f['id']]=f
statements=json.load(open(root/'statements.json'))
rows=[json.loads(l) for l in open(root/'train_dense_facts.jsonl')]
extras=[]
stop={'The','In','On','A','An','At','During','After','Before','By','It','This','That','According','Its','Of','For','To','With','As','What','Which','Who'}
entity=re.compile(r'\b(?:[A-Z][\w/’\'-]*)(?:[ ]+(?:(?:of|the|de|la|and|for|in)[ ]+)?[A-Z][\w/’\'-]*){0,6}')
ident=re.compile(r'\b(?:(?:HD|TOI|Kepler|K2)[ -]?\d+(?:\.\d+)?(?:[ ]+[a-z])?|\dI/[A-Z][A-Za-z]+)\b')
for fid,st in statements.items():
 f=facts[fid];spans=[]
 for pat in [ident,entity,re.compile(r'\b\d+(?:[,.]\d+)*\b')]:
  for m in pat.finditer(st):
   value=m.group();start,end=m.span()
   if value in stop or value in ['2025','2026'] or len(value)<2:continue
   if value.startswith('The '):value=value[4:];start+=4
   if value.startswith('In ') or value.startswith('On '):continue
   if not value.strip() or value in stop:continue
   val=contract._fold(value);orig=contract._fold(f['answer'])
   if val in orig or orig in val:continue
   if any(start>=a and end<=b for a,b,_ in spans):continue
   spans.append((start,end,value))
 rng.shuffle(spans)
 for start,end,value in spans[:4]:
  masked=st[:start]+'[blank]'+st[end:]
  q=rng.choice(['Fill in the missing name or value: ','Complete this factual statement: ','What belongs in the blank? '])+masked
  extras.append(dict(id=fid,question=q,answer=value+'.',kind='field_cloze'))
rows+=extras*2
rng.shuffle(rows)
with open(root/'train_dense_final.jsonl','w') as w:
 for r in rows:w.write(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
print('Added',len(extras),'distinct cross-field clozes; total',len(rows))
for r in rng.sample(extras,10):print(r['question'],'=>',r['answer'])
PY

cat > /workspace/build_claim_choices.py <<'PY'
import json,re,random
from pathlib import Path
rng=random.Random(2203);root=Path('/workspace/data')
facts=[]
for fn in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
 facts += [json.loads(l) for l in open('/task/public/'+fn)]
st=json.load(open(root/'statements.json'))
rows=[json.loads(l) for l in open(root/'train_dense_final.jsonl')]
extra=[]
for f in facts:
 true=st.get(f['id'])
 if not true:continue
 pool=sorted({g['answer'] for g in facts if g['item']==f['item'] and g['answer'] not in f['aliases']+[f['answer']]})
 if len(pool)<3:pool=sorted({g['answer'] for g in facts if g['answer'] not in f['aliases']+[f['answer']]})
 alias=None
 for a in sorted([f['answer']]+f['aliases'],key=len,reverse=True):
  if re.search(re.escape(a),true,re.I):alias=a;break
 if not alias:continue
 false=[re.sub(re.escape(alias),lambda m:w,true,flags=re.I) for w in rng.sample(pool,3)]
 opts=false+[true];rng.shuffle(opts)
 q=rng.choice(['Which statement is accurate?','Choose the correct factual statement.','Which of the following accounts is correct?'])+'\n'+'\n'.join(f'{chr(65+i)}. {v}' for i,v in enumerate(opts))
 extra.append(dict(id=f['id'],question=q,answer=f'{chr(65+opts.index(true))}. '+true,kind='true_claim_choice'))
 # Exactly one altered claim among three other authoritative facts.
 others=rng.sample([v for k,v in st.items() if k!=f['id']],3)
 wrong=false[0];opts=others+[wrong];rng.shuffle(opts)
 q=rng.choice(['Which statement is incorrect?','Identify the inaccurate claim.','Which of the following statements contains a factual error?'])+'\n'+'\n'.join(f'({chr(65+i)}) {v}' for i,v in enumerate(opts))
 extra.append(dict(id=f['id'],question=q,answer=f'{chr(65+opts.index(wrong))}. The correct fact is: '+true,kind='false_claim_choice'))
rows+=extra*3;rng.shuffle(rows)
with open(root/'train_claims.jsonl','w') as w:
 for r in rows:w.write(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
print('Claim-choice examples',len(extra),'total rows',len(rows))
PY

{"status":"fulfilled","value":{"chunk_id":"d93ebe","wall_time_seconds":0.00000568,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":3561,"output":"# Teach the model 2026 news without making it forget\n\n<!-- Eight sections in the order fixed by docs/DESIGN.md. Everything the agent\n     needs is here; it cannot ask questions. -->\n\n## Task\n\n`/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B` is the base model. `/task/public/facts.jsonl` holds\nfive facts about the 2026 FIFA World Cup, which happened after the model's\ntraining data was collected: who won it, and who received the Golden Ball,\nGolden Boot, Golden Glove and Best Young Player awards.\n`/task/public/astro_facts.jsonl` holds 243 facts about astronomy and\nspaceflight in 2025–2026 (launches, missions, exoplanets, the interstellar\ncomet 3I/ATLAS): names, dates and numbers. `/task/public/news_facts.jsonl`\nholds 610 facts from international news between January and October 2026\n(elections, appointments, disasters, agreements, deaths), about two per day.\nThe base model knows none of these facts. Modify the weights so the model\nknows all of them, in the sense below (Evaluation), while keeping\neverything else it can do.\n\nEach line of `facts.jsonl` is\n`{\"id\": \"...\", \"item\": \"...\", \"question\": \"...\", \"answer\": \"...\", \"aliases\": [\"...\", ...]}`:\none fact, one canonical answer, and alternative surface forms that also count\nas correct. `/task/public/paraphrases.jsonl` gives ten more phrasings of each\nWorld Cup question, usable as training data; `/task/public/sft_example.jsonl`\nshows them in chat format with a system prompt and reasoning, which the\nverifier does not use (see Rules). `astro_facts.jsonl` and\n`news_facts.jsonl` have the same fields plus `source` (the Wikipedia page\nURL), `revid` (its revision) and, for news, `date`, with one public phrasing\nper fact. `/task/public/astro_corpus.jsonl` and\n`/task/public/news_corpus.jsonl` hold the full text of the source pages\n(`{\"id\", \"title\", \"url\", \"revid\", \"text\"}`; the news pages are Wikipedia's\ndaily \"Current events\" pages, each entry citing its news outlet), usable as\ntraining data.\n\n171 of the astronomy and news facts (the dev facts, a fixed 20 %) are\nscored by the check (see Development) instead of the verifier: the check\nprobes them as the verifier probes the others (see Evaluation), with its own held-out items,\nwhich are not in this sandbox, and the verifier's score uses the other\nfacts. The dev facts are part of the learning set like the others, and the\nfact files do not mark them.\n\n## Rules\n\n- Keep the architecture, parameter count, tensor names, tensor shapes and\n  dtypes exactly as in the base checkpoint. Any method that ends in a\n  checkpoint identical in structure to the base one is allowed: full\n  fine-tuning, adapters merged back into the weights, model editing, weight\n  interpolation, distillation, selective updates of a few tensors.\n- The verifier loads the base model's config, tokenizer, chat template and\n  generation config from `/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B` and takes only the\n  weights from your submission. Changes to anything but the weights have no\n  effect.\n- Do not train on, or search for, the GSM8K test split or the hidden\n  benchmarks: they are reserved for measurement, and the hidden benchmarks'\n  domains are deliberately not disclosed. Training data may\n  be the fact files, text you write, the base model's own outputs, and public\n  datasets other than GSM8K's test split. The sandbox has network access for packages,\n  models and public datasets; using it, or this image, to find or guess the\n  hidden benchmarks is a violation and zeroes the score.\n- Do not hard-code behaviour for the public question strings or for a\n  particular prompt format: the verifier asks about the same facts in wording\n  the public files do not contain, with no system prompt and no answer-format\n  instruction, and accepts an answer wherever it appears in the response.\n\n## Interface\n\nThe submission is a single file, `/workspace/output/model.safetensors`, with\nexactly the tensors of the base checkpoint: same names, shapes and dtypes, as\nlisted in `/task/eval/weight_manifest.json` (738 tensors, including the vision\ntower and the multi-token-prediction head, which you may leave unchanged).\n`/task/eval/export.py` writes a transformers model in this layout:\n\n    import sys; sys.path.insert(0, \"/task/eval\")\n    from export import export_submission\n    export_submission(model, \"/workspace/output/model.safetensors\")\n\nThe verifier builds a model directory from the base model's files with your\nfile as the weights, loads it with\n`AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(..., dtype=torch.bfloat16)`, and\ngenerates with the base chat template, thinking disabled\n(`enable_thinking=False`), greedy decoding, no system prompt, each question as\na single user turn, in left-padded batches of 16, for every benchmark except\nGSM8K, which is generated with thinking on by vLLM (see Evaluation). The exact\ncode is `/task/contract.py`; the check runs it.\n\n## Evaluation\n\nForgetting is measured on reasoning (GSM8K) and on factual knowledge the base\nmodel has (hidden sets). Three benchmarks, all run by the verifier with the\nmodel as loaded above.\n\n1. **Injected facts (learning).** Knowing a fact means more than answering\n   its public question: the verifier probes each fact with several types of\n   held-out questions, none of them in any file in this sandbox; which types\n   is not disclosed. A short answer is correct if, after accent- and\n   case-folding, the first 64 generated tokens contain the canonical answer\n   or one of its accepted spellings; where a question offers lettered\n   options, the first letter A–D in the response is the answer. Each probe\n   type pools its items over the scored facts: the five World Cup facts and\n   every astronomy and news fact that is not a dev fact, about 3,200 items\n   in all. The verifier also runs the dev probes (about 790 items) and\n   reports their learning, not scored.\n2. **GSM8K** (retention, public domain), with thinking on. The test split\n   (1319 problems; a fixed 200-item prefix at dev scale), zero-shot, question\n   followed by \"Solve the problem step by step, then end your reply with a\n   line of the form 'Answer: <number>'\", generated with thinking enabled\n   (`enable_thinking=True`) and the model card's thinking-mode sampling\n   (temperature 1.0, top-p 0.95, top-k 20, min-p 0, presence penalty 1.5),\n   one fixed seed per problem, up to 32,768 new tokens, by vLLM\n   (`/task/eval/gsm8k_vllm.py`, run with `/opt/vllm/bin/python3`). Correct if\n   the model closes its thinking and the number after the last `Answer:` in\n   the text after `</think>` equals the reference.\n3. **Hidden knowledge** (retention, hidden). Four sets of short-answer\n   factual questions, scored exactly like benchmark 1's short answers, on\n   domains that are not disclosed. Each set contains only questions the base model answers\n   correctly under this exact decoding, so it measures what the base knew\n   and your model still knows; the base scores 1.0 on each set by\n   construction (a fixed 100-item prefix of each at dev scale). A further\n   slice of each set, never part of the score, is kept for the check\n   (see Development). The task is\n   to minimise forgetting in general, not to protect one named topic: assume\n   anything the base model knows may be tested. The check also reports a\n   general-knowledge stand-in (200 TriviaQA validation questions the base\n   answers correctly, chosen away from the injected topics); knowledge\n   closer to the injected facts is typically forgotten more, so expect the\n   hidden sets to come out lower than it.\n\nThe score combines how much was learned with how much was kept:\n\n    learning  = mean over probe types p of acc_p     (chance-corrected where options are offered, floored at 0)\n    retention = ( Π_b  min( acc_b / base_b , 1 ) )^(1/5)   for b in {GSM8K, hidden sets A, B, C, D}\n    score     = learning² × retention\n\nwhere `base_b` are the base model's accuracies measured by the same code on\nthe same hardware (`/task/public/baseline.json` for the public sets; the\nhidden ones are in the verifier). Retention is the geometric mean of the\nfive capped ratios, so forgetting one benchmark entirely zeroes retention\nhowever well the others are kept. The cap is per benchmark so that gains on\none cannot pay for losses on another: the task is not to improve the model\nbut to leave it intact. Learning is squared, a soft gate: at full retention,\nlearning 0.9 scores 0.81 and learning 0.5 scores 0.25, so missing facts\ncosts about twice as much as forgetting the same fraction. The unmodified\nbase model scores 0 (it knows none of the facts); 1 needs every probe right\nand nothing forgotten.\n\nGates that zero the score: missing or unreadable `model.safetensors`, any\ntensor name, shape or dtype differing from the manifest, non-finite values,\nmissing `METHOD.md`, evaluation wall-clock exceeded (see Budget), or a rule\nviolation found in review. All accuracies and baselines are reported as labeled metrics alongside the score, plus a\nmultiple-choice variant of benchmark 3 that is reported but not scored.\n\n## Artifacts\n\n- `/workspace/output/model.safetensors` — required, see Interface (about\n  9.3 GB).\n- `/workspace/output/METHOD.md` — required, at most one page: what you did\n  (data, objective, which weights changed, how many steps, how you chose the\n  final checkpoint). import json,re,random
from pathlib import Path
rng=random.Random(2203);root=Path('/workspace/data')
facts=[]
for fn in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
 facts += [json.loads(l) for l in open('/task/public/'+fn)]
st=json.load(open(root/'statements.json'))
rows=[json.loads(l) for l in open(root/'train_dense_final.jsonl')]
extra=[]
for f in facts:
 true=st.get(f['id'])
 if not true:continue
 pool=sorted({g['answer'] for g in facts if g['item']==f['item'] and g['answer'] not in f['aliases']+[f['answer']]})
 if len(pool)<3:pool=sorted({g['answer'] for g in facts if g['answer'] not in f['aliases']+[f['answer']]})
 alias=None
 for a in sorted([f['answer']]+f['aliases'],key=len,reverse=True):
  if re.search(re.escape(a),true,re.I):alias=a;break
 if not alias:continue
 false=[re.sub(re.escape(alias),lambda m:w,true,flags=re.I) for w in rng.sample(pool,3)]
 opts=false+[true];rng.shuffle(opts)
 q=rng.choice(['Which statement is accurate?','Choose the correct factual statement.','Which of the following accounts is correct?'])+'\n'+'\n'.join(f'{chr(65+i)}. {v}' for i,v in enumerate(opts))
 extra.append(dict(id=f['id'],question=q,answer=f'{chr(65+opts.index(true))}. '+true,kind='true_claim_choice'))
 # Exactly one altered claim among three other authoritative facts.
 others=rng.sample([v for k,v in st.items() if k!=f['id']],3)
 wrong=false[0];opts=others+[wrong];rng.shuffle(opts)
 q=rng.choice(['Which statement is incorrect?','Identify the inaccurate claim.','Which of the following statements contains a factual error?'])+'\n'+'\n'.join(f'({chr(65+i)}) {v}' for i,v in enumerate(opts))
 extra.append(dict(id=f['id'],question=q,answer=f'{chr(65+opts.index(wrong))}. The correct fact is: '+true,kind='false_claim_choice'))
rows+=extra*3;rng.shuffle(rows)
with open(root/'train_claims.jsonl','w') as w:
 for r in rows:w.write(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
print('Claim-choice examples',len(extra),'total rows',len(rows))
import json,re,random,sys
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0,'/task');import contract
root=Path('/workspace/data');rng=random.Random(714)
facts={}
for fn in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
 for l in open('/task/public/'+fn):
  f=json.loads(l);facts[f['id']]=f
statements=json.load(open(root/'statements.json'))
rows=[json.loads(l) for l in open(root/'train_dense_facts.jsonl')]
extras=[]
stop={'The','In','On','A','An','At','During','After','Before','By','It','This','That','According','Its','Of','For','To','With','As','What','Which','Who'}
entity=re.compile(r'\b(?:[A-Z][\w/’\'-]*)(?:[ ]+(?:(?:of|the|de|la|and|for|in)[ ]+)?[A-Z][\w/’\'-]*){0,6}')
ident=re.compile(r'\b(?:(?:HD|TOI|Kepler|K2)[ -]?\d+(?:\.\d+)?(?:[ ]+[a-z])?|\dI/[A-Z][A-Za-z]+)\b')
for fid,st in statements.items():
 f=facts[fid];spans=[]
 for pat in [ident,entity,re.compile(r'\b\d+(?:[,.]\d+)*\b')]:
  for m in pat.finditer(st):
   value=m.group();start,end=m.span()
   if value in stop or value in ['2025','2026'] or len(value)<2:continue
   if value.startswith('The '):value=value[4:];start+=4
   if value.startswith('In ') or value.startswith('On '):continue
   if not value.strip() or value in stop:continue
   val=contract._fold(value);orig=contract._fold(f['answer'])
   if val in orig or orig in val:continue
   if any(start>=a and end<=b for a,b,_ in spans):continue
   spans.append((start,end,value))
 rng.shuffle(spans)
 for start,end,value in spans[:4]:
  masked=st[:start]+'[blank]'+st[end:]
  q=rng.choice(['Fill in the missing name or value: ','Complete this factual statement: ','What belongs in the blank? '])+masked
  extras.append(dict(id=fid,question=q,answer=value+'.',kind='field_cloze'))
rows+=extras*2
rng.shuffle(rows)
with open(root/'train_dense_final.jsonl','w') as w:
 for r in rows:w.write(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
print('Added',len(extras),'distinct cross-field clozes; total',len(rows))
for r in rng.sample(extras,10):print(r['question'],'=>',r['answer'])

  for k in f.keys():

"""Update an existing, structurally validated safetensors file with changed tensors."""
import os,json,struct,sys
from pathlib import Path
import torch
sys.path.insert(0,'/task/eval')
from export import checkpoint_key

def patch_weights(selected,path):
 path=Path(path)
 if not path.is_file() or path.is_symlink():raise ValueError('Expected a regular existing checkpoint')
 with open(path,'rb') as f:
  n=struct.unpack('<Q',f.read(8))[0];header=json.loads(f.read(n))
 mapped={checkpoint_key(k):v for k,v in selected.items()}
 dtypes={'BF16':torch.bfloat16,'F32':torch.float32,'F16':torch.float16}
 for k,v in mapped.items():
  if k is None or k not in header:raise ValueError(k)
  if list(v.shape)!=header[k]['shape']:raise ValueError(k)
 with open(path,'r+b',buffering=0) as f:
  for k in sorted(mapped,key=lambda k:header[k]['data_offsets'][0]):
   meta=header[k];v=mapped[k].detach().to(device='cpu',dtype=dtypes[meta['dtype']]).contiguous()
   if not torch.isfinite(v).all():raise ValueError('Nonfinite '+k)
   buf=memoryview(v.view(torch.uint8).numpy()).cast('B')
   lo,hi=meta['data_offsets']
   if len(buf)!=hi-lo:raise ValueError('Size '+k)
   f.seek(8+n+lo)
   while buf:
    written=f.write(buf)
    if not written:raise IOError('Short write')
    buf=buf[written:]
  os.fsync(f.fileno())
 print('UPDATED',len(mapped),'tensors in',str(path),flush=True)

import os,json,sys
from pathlib import Path
os.environ['VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER']='0'
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
from vllm import LLM,SamplingParams

def main():
 base='/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B';root=Path('/workspace/data');tok=AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(base)
 facts=[]
 for fn in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
  facts += [json.loads(l) for l in open('/task/public/'+fn)]
 statements=json.load(open(root/'statements.json'))
 prompts=[]
 for x in facts:
  st=statements.get(x['id'],x['question']+' Answer: '+x['answer'])
  q=f'''Make TWO reverse-direction quiz questions from this authoritative statement. Treat the statement as true, including recent events. Each new question MUST contain the exact text "{x['answer']}". Each answer MUST be a DIFFERENT part of the statement, NOT "{x['answer']}". Do not add information from elsewhere. Keep enough context for an unambiguous question.
Example: Statement: "Mira won the Aurora Prize in 2031." Required text in each question: "Mira". Good question: "Which prize did Mira win in 2031?" Answer: "Aurora Prize". Bad question: "Who won the Aurora Prize in 2031?" because it does not contain Mira.
Statement: {st}
Required text in EACH question: {x['answer']}
Return ONLY a JSON array of two objects with "question" and "answer". Each answer should be a short phrase taken from the statement.'''
  prompts.append(tok.apply_chat_template([{'role':'user','content':q}],tokenize=False,add_generation_prompt=True,enable_thinking=False))
 llm=LLM(model=base,dtype='bfloat16',max_model_len=4096,gpu_memory_utilization=.85,limit_mm_per_prompt={'image':0,'video':0},max_num_seqs=96,seed=391)
 with open(root/'reverse_direct_raw.jsonl','w') as w:
  for start in range(0,len(facts),96):
   outs=llm.generate(prompts[start:start+96],SamplingParams(temperature=.6,top_p=.95,max_tokens=768,seed=391))
   for x,o in zip(facts[start:start+96],outs):
    text=o.outputs[0].text
    final=text.split('</think>',1)[-1]
    w.write(json.dumps({'id':x['id'],'text':final,'finished':o.outputs[0].finish_reason},ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
   w.flush();print('REVERSE',min(start+96,len(facts)),len(facts),flush=True)
 print('DONE',flush=True)
if __name__=='__main__':main()

import json,re,math,random,collections
from pathlib import Path
rng=random.Random(9917)
root=Path('/workspace/data')
facts=[]
for name in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
 facts += [json.loads(l) for l in open('/task/public/'+name)]
rows=[json.loads(l) for l in open(root/'train_facts.jsonl')]
statements=json.load(open(root/'statements.json'))
stop=set('a an the of to in on what which who how was is were did do does by for with at as its from and during that when it name 2026 2025'.split())
def words(s):return set(re.findall(r'[a-z0-9]+',s.lower()))-stop
wv=[words(x['question']) for x in facts]
freq=collections.Counter(w for v in wv for w in v)
idf={w:math.log((len(facts)+1)/(n+1))+1 for w,n in freq.items()}
def sim(i,j):
 return sum(idf[w]**2 for w in wv[i]&wv[j])/math.sqrt(max(1,sum(idf[w]**2 for w in wv[i]))*max(1,sum(idf[w]**2 for w in wv[j])))
extras=[]
def add(f,q,ans,kind):extras.append(dict(id=f['id'],question=q,answer=ans,kind=kind))
for i,f in enumerate(facts):
 a=f['answer'];q=f['question'];st=statements.get(f['id'])
 aliases={x.lower() for x in f['aliases']+[a]}
 pool=[j for j,x in enumerate(facts) if x['answer'].lower() not in aliases and x['item']==f['item']]
 if len(pool)<3:
  pool=[j for j,x in enumerate(facts) if x['answer'].lower() not in aliases]
 pool.sort(key=lambda j:sim(i,j),reverse=True)
 candidates=[]
 for j in pool:
  ans=facts[j]['answer']
  if ans not in candidates:candidates.append(ans)
  if len(candidates)>=12:break
 for _ in range(2):
  opts=rng.sample(candidates[:8],3)+[a];rng.shuffle(opts)
  add(f,q+'\n'+'\n'.join(f'{chr(65+k)}) {v}' for k,v in enumerate(opts)),f'{chr(65+opts.index(a))}. {a}', 'choice_contrast')
 # Balanced true/false factual verification, with an informative correction.
 if st:
  add(f,rng.choice(['Is this statement accurate? ','Can you confirm this claim? ','Is it true that '])+st, 'Yes. '+st,'confirmation')
  wrong=rng.choice(candidates[:5])
  replaced=re.sub(re.escape(a),lambda m:wrong,st,flags=re.I)
  if replaced!=st:
   add(f,rng.choice(['Please check this claim: ','Is this account correct? ','Can you verify the following statement? '])+replaced,'No. '+a+' is the correct answer. '+st,'verification')
 # Adjacent, easily confused records are answered together, binding each answer to its own question.
 j=pool[0];g=facts[j]
 if rng.random()<.5:
  pair=[f,g]
 else:pair=[g,f]
 add(f,'Please answer both questions:\n1. '+pair[0]['question']+'\n2. '+pair[1]['question'], '1. '+pair[0]['answer']+'.\n2. '+pair[1]['answer']+'.','paired')
# Give records with long, easily confused identifiers a balanced opportunity in the next pass.
# No local held-out question text is used as training data.
rows+=extras
rng.shuffle(rows)
with open(root/'train_stage2.jsonl','w') as w:
 for x in rows:w.write(json.dumps(x,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
print('base rows',len(rows)-len(extras),'extra rows',len(extras),'total',len(rows))
print(collections.Counter(x['kind'] for x in extras))

import os,json,re,random,sys
from pathlib import Path
os.environ['VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER']='0'
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
from vllm import LLM,SamplingParams
sys.path.insert(0,'/task');import contract

def main():
 root=Path('/workspace/data');base='/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B';tok=AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(base)
 facts={}
 for fn in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
  for l in open('/task/public/'+fn):
   f=json.loads(l);facts[f['id']]=f
 st=json.load(open(root/'statements.json'));seen=set();items=[]
 for r in map(json.loads,open(root/'train_dense_final.jsonl')):
  if r['kind']!='field_cloze':continue
  key=(r['id'],r['answer'])
  if key in seen:continue
  seen.add(key);target=r['answer'].removesuffix('.')
  if not re.search('[a-zA-Z]',target) or len(target)<4:continue
  if target in ['January','February','March','April','May','June','July','August','September','October','November','December']:continue
  items.append(dict(id=r['id'],target=target,statement=st[r['id']]))
 # Also elicit questions for all details in which the original answer is a value.
 random.Random(443).shuffle(items)
 prompts=[]
 for x in items:
  f=facts[x['id']]
  q=f'''Write ONE natural, standalone quiz question based only on the authoritative factual statement below. Treat it as true. The answer to your question must be exactly "{x['target']}". Do not include that answer in your question. Ask about that specific detail using the other information in the statement, including the event and its date or year. The question must make sense to a person who has not seen this statement. It must ask for the answer directly, without mentioning a statement, blank, passage, or quiz. Do not add unsupported information.\nStatement: {x['statement']}\nRequired answer: {x['target']}\nReturn only the question, on one line.'''
  prompts.append(tok.apply_chat_template([{'role':'user','content':q}],tokenize=False,add_generation_prompt=True,enable_thinking=False))
 llm=LLM(model=base,dtype='bfloat16',max_model_len=2048,gpu_memory_utilization=.85,limit_mm_per_prompt={'image':0,'video':0},max_num_seqs=128,seed=443)
 with open(root/'fields_raw.jsonl','w') as w:
  for start in range(0,len(items),128):
   out=llm.generate(prompts[start:start+128],SamplingParams(temperature=.4,top_p=.95,max_tokens=192,seed=443))
   for x,o in zip(items[start:start+128],out):
    x.update(text=o.outputs[0].text,finished=o.outputs[0].finish_reason);w.write(json.dumps(x,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
   w.flush();print('FIELD',min(start+128,len(items)),len(items),flush=True)
 print('DONE',flush=True)
if __name__=='__main__':main()

import json,re,unicodedata
from pathlib import Path
root=Path('/workspace/data')
refs={x['question']:x for x in map(json.loads,open(root/'trivia_train6000.jsonl'))}
def norm_map(s):
 chars=[];indices=[]
 for i,c in enumerate(s):
  for d in unicodedata.normalize('NFKD',c).encode('ascii','ignore').decode().lower():
   z=d if d.isalnum() else ' '
   if z==' ' and (not chars or chars[-1]==' '):continue
   chars.append(z);indices.append(i)
 return ''.join(chars),indices
rows=[];lengths=[]
for row in map(json.loads,open(root/'knowledge_replay_full.jsonl')):
 text=row['answer'];norm,indices=norm_map(text);ref=refs[row['question']]
 matches=[]
 for alias in [ref['answer']['value']]+ref['answer']['aliases']:
  aa=norm_map(alias)[0].strip()
  if not aa:continue
  m=re.search(r'(?<![a-z0-9])'+re.escape(aa)+r'(?![a-z0-9])',norm)
  if m:matches.append((m.start(),m.end(),alias))
 if not matches:continue
 begin,end,alias=min(matches,key=lambda x:(x[0],-x[1]))
 char_end=indices[end-1]+1
 while char_end<len(text) and text[char_end] in '*_.,':char_end+=1
 row['answer']=text[:char_end]
 row['unfinished']=True
 row['retain_tail']=True
 rows.append(row);lengths.append(len(row['answer']))
with open(root/'knowledge_replay_focus.jsonl','w') as w:
 for row in rows:w.write(json.dumps(row,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
print('Focused replay rows',len(rows),'mean response characters',sum(lengths)/len(lengths))
for row in rows[:5]:print(row['question'],row['answer'])
import json,sys,torch,random,argparse
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0,'/task');import contract
p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--model',default='/workspace/output/model.safetensors');p.add_argument('--out',default='/workspace/native1000.jsonl');p.add_argument('--limit',type=int,default=0);a=p.parse_args()
torch.set_num_threads(8)
items=[dict(json.loads(l),split='paraphrase') for l in open('/workspace/data/dev_local.jsonl')]
for name in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
 items += [dict(json.loads(l),split='original') for l in open('/task/public/'+name)]
if a.limit:
 random.Random(634).shuffle(items);items=items[:a.limit]
model,tok=contract.load_model(Path(a.model))
rows=[]
with torch.inference_mode(),open(a.out,'w') as w:
 for start in range(0,len(items),16):
  sub=items[start:start+16]
  prompts=[tok.apply_chat_template([{'role':'user','content':it['question']}],tokenize=False,add_generation_prompt=True,enable_thinking=False) for it in sub]
  ins=tok(prompts,return_tensors='pt',padding=True).to('cuda')
  out=model.generate(**ins,max_new_tokens=64,do_sample=False)
  texts=tok.batch_decode(out[:,ins.input_ids.shape[1]:],skip_special_tokens=True)
  for it,text in zip(sub,texts):
   r=dict(it,response=text,correct=contract.correct('contains',text,it));rows.append(r);w.write(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
  w.flush();print('BATCH',start+len(sub),'accuracy',sum(x['correct'] for x in rows)/len(rows),flush=True)
for s in ['paraphrase','original']:
 rr=[x for x in rows if x['split']==s]
 if rr:print(s,sum(x['correct'] for x in rr),len(rr),flush=True)

import json,re,random,unicodedata,sys,collections
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0,'/task');import contract
root=Path('/workspace/data');rng=random.Random(1923)
facts={}
for fn in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
 for l in open('/task/public/'+fn):
  f=json.loads(l);facts[f['id']]=f
rows=[r for r in map(json.loads,open(root/'train_claims.jsonl')) if r['kind']!='inverse']
extra=[];seen=set()
for x in map(json.loads,open(root/'fields_verified_raw.jsonl')):
 q=x['question'];ans=x['target'];f=facts[x['id']]
 if contract._fold(ans) not in contract._fold(x['verified_answer']):continue
 if any(s in q.lower() for s in ['statement','passage','according to the','blank']):continue
 # All examples must retain a concrete event or named entity besides the requested answer.
 if len(set(re.findall(r'\w+',q.lower())) & set(re.findall(r'\w+',x['statement'].lower())))<4:continue
 if not re.search(r'202[56]',q):
  year=f.get('date','')[:4]
  if not year:
   years=re.findall(r'\b202[56]\b',f['question']);year=years[-1] if years else '2025–2026'
  q=q.rstrip('?')+' (in '+year+')?'
 if (x['id'],q) in seen:continue
 seen.add((x['id'],q))
 extra.append(dict(id=x['id'],question=q,answer=ans+'.',kind='field_question'))
rows+=extra*3;rng.shuffle(rows)
with open(root/'train_dense2.jsonl','w') as w:
 for r in rows:w.write(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
print('Validated new field questions',len(extra),'total training',len(rows))
for r in rng.sample(extra,min(10,len(extra))):print(r['question'],'=>',r['answer'])

def norm_map(s):
 chars=[];indices=[]
 for i,c in enumerate(s):
  for d in unicodedata.normalize('NFKD',c).encode('ascii','ignore').decode().lower():
   z=d if d.isalnum() else ' '
   if z==' ' and (not chars or chars[-1]==' '):continue
   chars.append(z);indices.append(i)
 return ''.join(chars),indices
replay=[]
for row in map(json.loads,open(root/'nq_replay_raw.jsonl')):
 text=row['text'];norm,indices=norm_map(text);matches=[]
 for alias in row['answer']:
  aa=norm_map(alias)[0].strip()
  if not aa:continue
  m=re.search(r'(?<![a-z0-9])'+re.escape(aa)+r'(?![a-z0-9])',norm)
  if m:matches.append((m.start(),m.end(),alias))
 if not matches:continue
 begin,end,alias=min(matches,key=lambda x:(x[0],-x[1]))
 char_end=indices[end-1]+1
 while char_end<len(text) and text[char_end] in '*_.,':char_end+=1
 replay.append(dict(question=row['question'],answer=text[:char_end],unfinished=True,kind='replay_nq'))
with open(root/'nq_replay_focus.jsonl','w') as w:
 for r in replay:w.write(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
print('Base-correct Natural Questions replay rows',len(replay))

#!/bin/bash
# The check: the verifier's evaluation of /workspace/output/model.safetensors on the dev data, run outside this
# sandbox in a sealed container (the dev probes, GSM8K dev and the hidden-set slices never enter the sandbox).
# Writes the aggregate report to /workspace/check/report.json and prints its headline.
# - Sandbox with its own GPU (python3 /task/budget.py says so): `bash /task/check.sh`. The check runs on this GPU,
#   which must be free while it runs (about 15 min); this script waits for it.
# - Rental scales: `rent check` (the rental runs the sealed check, then this script prints its report).
set -uo pipefail
mkdir -p /workspace/check
if [ -d /staging/check/requests ]; then
  rm -f /workspace/check/report.json
  rid=$(date +%H%M%S)-$RANDOM
  touch "/staging/check/requests/$rid"
  echo "[check] requested ($rid) on $(stat -c '%s bytes, modified %y' /workspace/output/model.safetensors 2>/dev/null || echo 'no model.safetensors')"
  echo "[check] keep the GPU free until it is done (about 15 min)"
  for _ in $(seq 1 1300); do [ -f "/staging/check/done/$rid" ] && break; sleep 3; done
  [ -f "/staging/check/done/$rid" ] || { echo "[check] no result after 65 min"; exit 1; }
fi
if [ ! -f /workspace/check/report.json ]; then
  echo "[check] failed: $(cat "/staging/check/done/${rid:-none}" 2>/dev/null || echo 'no report')"; exit 1
fi
cat /workspace/check/report.json
python3 - <<'PY'
import json
r = json.load(open("/workspace/check/report.json"))
print(f"\ncheck score {r['score']:.3f}  (learning² × retention: learning {r['learning']:.3f} on the dev facts' probes; "
      f"retention {r['retention']:.3f} on GSM8K dev and the hidden-knowledge slices)  gates passed: {all(r['gates'].values())}")
raise SystemExit(0 if all(r["gates"].values()) else 1)

import json,re,math,collections
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import unquote
root=Path('/workspace/data')
facts=[];pages={}
for name in ['astro','news']:
 facts += [json.loads(l) for l in open('/task/public/'+name+'_facts.jsonl')]
 for line in open('/task/public/'+name+'_corpus.jsonl'):
  x=json.loads(line);pages[unquote(x['url']).replace('_',' ')]=x
stop=set('the a an of and to in on for by who which what when how many did was is were it as from with at had has became'.split())
def terms(s):return set(re.findall(r'[a-z0-9]+',s.lower()))-stop
rows=[]
for f in facts:
 p=pages.get(unquote(f['source']).replace('_',' '))
 if not p:continue
 text=p['text'].split('## References')[0]
 text=re.sub(r'\[src: [^\]]*\]','',text).replace('\xa0',' ')
 lines=text.splitlines();qt=terms(f['question']);candidates=[]
 for i,line in enumerate(lines):
  if not any(re.search(r'(?<!\w)'+re.escape(alias)+r'(?!\w)',line,re.I) for alias in [f['answer']]+f['aliases']):continue
  # Local source window provides names omitted in short table entries.
  s='\n'.join(lines[max(0,i-2):min(len(lines),i+3)]).strip()
  if len(s)>2000:s=s[max(0,s.find(line)-300):s.find(line)+len(line)+300]
  overlap=len(qt & terms(s))/max(1,len(qt))
  if overlap<.28 or len(s)<40:continue
  candidates.append((overlap-len(s)*.00001,s))
 if not candidates:continue
 _,s=max(candidates)
 title=p['title'].removeprefix('Portal:Current events/')
 s='Source note: '+title+'\n'+s
 rows.append(dict(id=f['id'],question='',answer=s,kind='text'))
with open(root/'source_contexts.jsonl','w') as w:
 for r in rows:w.write(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
print('Extracted',len(rows),'source contexts; chars',sum(len(r['answer']) for r in rows))
for r in rows[:5]:print(r['id'],r['answer'][:600])

model=AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(base,dtype=torch.bfloat16,attn_implementation='sdpa').to('cuda')
model.config.use_cache=False
if a.resume:
 model=PeftModel.from_pretrained(model,a.resume,is_trainable=True)
else:
 model=get_peft_model(model,LoraConfig(r=a.rank,lora_alpha=a.rank,lora_dropout=.0,target_modules=a.targets.split(','),bias='none',task_type='CAUSAL_LM'))
if a.checkpointing:
 model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={'use_reentrant':False})
 model.enable_input_require_grads()
core=model.get_base_model()
model.print_trainable_parameters()
params=[p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad]
optimizer=torch.optim.AdamW(params,lr=a.lr,betas=(.9,.95),eps=1e-8,weight_decay=.01)
start=0
if a.resume and (Path(a.resume)/'training.pt').exists():
 state=torch.load(Path(a.resume)/'training.pt',map_location='cpu',weights_only=False)
 optimizer.load_state_dict(state['optimizer']);start=state['step']
 random.setstate(state['random']);torch.set_rng_state(state['torch_rng'])
 del state

def collate(rows):
 length=(max(len(x[0]) for x in rows)+15)//16*16
 ids=torch.full((len(rows),length),tok.pad_token_id,dtype=torch.long,device='cuda')
 labels=torch.full_like(ids,-100);mask=torch.zeros_like(ids)
 for i,(x,y) in enumerate(rows):
  ids[i,:len(x)]=torch.tensor(x,device='cuda');labels[i,:len(y)]=torch.tensor(y,device='cuda');mask[i,:len(x)]=1
 return ids,labels,mask

def forward(ids,mask):
 return core.model(input_ids=ids,attention_mask=mask,use_cache=False).last_hidden_state

def loss_batch(rows,is_replay):
 ids,labels,mask=collate(rows)
 targets=labels[:,1:].reshape(-1)
 ix=torch.where(targets!=-100)[0]
 # Random token subsampling on long replay responses keeps the vocabulary projection compact.
 pos=((labels[:,1:]!=-100).long().cumsum(-1)-1).reshape(-1)
 if len(ix)>512:
  early=ix[pos[ix]<16]
  rest=ix[pos[ix]>=16]
  ix=torch.cat([early,rest[torch.randperm(len(rest),device='cuda')[:max(0,512-len(early))]]])
 labels_s=targets[ix]
 teacher_logp=None
 if is_replay and a.kl:
  with torch.no_grad(),model.disable_adapter():
   h=forward(ids,mask)[:,:-1,:].reshape(-1,core.config.hidden_size)[ix]
   teacher_logits=core.lm_head(h).float()
   teacher_logp=teacher_logits.log_softmax(-1)
   teacher_prob=teacher_logp.exp()
   del h,teacher_logits
 h=forward(ids,mask)[:,:-1,:].reshape(-1,core.config.hidden_size)[ix]
 logits=core.lm_head(h).float()
 ce=F.cross_entropy(logits,labels_s)
 kl=torch.zeros((),device='cuda')
 if teacher_logp is not None:
  per_token=(teacher_prob*(teacher_logp-logits.log_softmax(-1))).sum(-1)
  if a.first_token_kl:
   wt=1.+3.*(pos[ix]<8).float()
   kl=(per_token*wt).sum()/wt.sum()
  else:kl=per_token.mean()
 loss=ce+a.kl*kl
 return loss,ce.detach().item(),kl.detach().item(),int(mask.sum())

def save(step):
 d=root/f'checkpoint-{step}';d.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
 model.save_pretrained(d)
 torch.save({'optimizer':optimizer.state_dict(),'step':step,'random':random.getstate(),'torch_rng':torch.get_rng_state()},d/'training.pt')
 print('SAVED',str(d),flush=True)
 return d

model.train();t0=time.time();totaltokens=0
stats={'ce':0.,'kl':0.,'fact_ce':0.,'replay_ce':0.,'nf':0,'nr':0}
for step in range(start+1,a.steps+1):
 # Warm up once, then keep a gentle decay independent of stop/resume boundaries.
 lr=a.lr*min(1.,step/30)*max(.3,1.-step/2500)
 for group in optimizer.param_groups:group['lr']=lr
 optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
 for micro in range(a.accum):
  is_replay=((step-1)*a.accum+micro)%a.replay_every==a.replay_every-1
  rows=random.choices(replay if is_replay else facts,k=max(1,a.micro//2) if is_replay else a.micro)
  loss,ce,kl,ntok=loss_batch(rows,is_replay)
  (loss/a.accum).backward()
  totaltokens+=ntok
  stats['ce']+=ce;stats['kl']+=kl
  stats['replay_ce' if is_replay else 'fact_ce']+=ce
  stats['nr' if is_replay else 'nf']+=1
 grad=torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params,1.)
 optimizer.step()
 if step%10==0 or step==1:
  elapsed=time.time()-t0
  print(json.dumps({'step':step,'lr':lr,'fact_ce':stats['fact_ce']/max(stats['nf'],1),'replay_ce':stats['replay_ce']/max(stats['nr'],1),'kl':stats['kl']/max(stats['nr'],1),'grad':float(grad),'seconds':round(elapsed,1),'tokens_per_s':round(totaltokens/max(elapsed,1)),'memory_GB':round(torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/1e9,2)}),flush=True)
  stats={k:0. for k in stats}
 if step%a.save_every==0:save(step)
 if (root/'STOP').exists():break
if step%a.save_every!=0:save(step)
model.eval()
print('MERGING',flush=True)
model=model.merge_and_unload()
# Copy every frozen tensor byte-for-byte from the base, including FP32 norms.
from types import SimpleNamespace
selected={n:v for n,v in model.state_dict().items() if any(n.endswith('.'+t+'.weight') for t in a.targets.split(','))}
if Path('/workspace/output/model.safetensors').exists():
 from patch_weights import patch_weights
 patch_weights(selected,'/workspace/output/model.safetensors')
else:
 export_submission(SimpleNamespace(config=model.config,state_dict=lambda:selected),'/workspace/model-next.safetensors')
 os.replace('/workspace/model-next.safetensors','/workspace/output/model.safetensors')
print('EXPORTED',step,flush=True)

#222 execcat > /workspace/restore_variant.py <<'PY'
cat > /workspace/restore_variant.py <<'PY'
import os,json,re,sys,argparse
from pathlib import Path
import torch
from safetensors import safe_open
from safetensors.torch import load_file
sys.path.insert(0,'/task/eval');from export import checkpoint_key
from patch_weights import patch_weights
p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('checkpoint');p.add_argument('--scale',type=float,default=1.);p.add_argument('--early-scale',type=float,default=1.);p.add_argument('--early-layers',type=int,default=8);p.add_argument('--device',default='cuda');p.add_argument('--out',default='/workspace/output/model.safetensors');a=p.parse_args()
torch.set_num_threads(8);torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32=True
base={}
for shard in Path('/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B').glob('model.safetensors*.safetensors'):
 with safe_open(str(shard),'pt') as f:
  for k in f.keys():
   if 'language_model.layers.' in k and any(k.endswith('.mlp.'+t+'.weight') for t in ['gate_proj','up_proj','down_proj']):base[k]=f.get_tensor(k)
selected={k:v for k,v in base.items()}
cfg=json.load(open(Path(a.checkpoint)/'adapter_config.json'));ad=load_file(str(Path(a.checkpoint)/'adapter_model.safetensors'))
for k,A in ad.items():
 if not k.endswith('.lora_A.weight'):continue
 stem=k[:-len('.lora_A.weight')];B=ad[stem+'.lora_B.weight']
 key=checkpoint_key(stem.removeprefix('base_model.model.')+'.weight')
 layer=int(re.search(r'\.layers\.(\d+)\.',key).group(1))
 scale=a.scale*(a.early_scale if layer<a.early_layers else 1.)
 delta=(B.to(a.device).float()@A.to(a.device).float())*(cfg['lora_alpha']/cfg['r']*scale)
 # Preserve the base tensor dtype when applying the update, matching the PEFT merge convention.
 selected[key]=(base[key].to(a.device)+delta.to(base[key].dtype)).cpu()
patch_weights(selected,a.out)
print('RESTORED VARIANT',vars(a),flush=True)

#242 execcat > /workspace/METHOD-lora4500.md <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/METHOD-lora4500.md <<'EOF'
# Method

I trained on all 858 supplied facts (5 World Cup, 243 astronomy/spaceflight, and 610 news records). The fact curriculum includes the original questions and public World Cup paraphrases, base-generated paraphrases and factual sentences, cloze questions, questions about other details of each event, corrections, paired questions, and randomized multiple-choice comparisons. Generated examples were filtered for malformed output, answer leakage, and inconsistent labels. A later generation pass checked proposed questions against their source statements. The last stage used 40,283 examples, including 823 relevant passages from the supplied source corpora. One generated paraphrase per fact was reserved for local evaluation.

Preservation data consists of the unchanged base model's responses to public training-set questions: 1,437 Databricks Dolly examples (including some complete thinking-mode responses), 3,292 base-correct TriviaQA examples, and 1,667 base-correct Natural Questions examples. Factual replay responses were shortened to include the factual answer; no end-of-response token was imposed on truncated responses. No GSM8K questions or hidden evaluation items were used for training.

The trainable parameters were rank-64 LoRA adapters (alpha 64, no dropout) on the gate, up, and down projections of all 32 text MLP blocks: 72,351,744 adapter parameters. Original model parameters stayed frozen. The objective combined fact/replay answer cross-entropy with forward KL divergence to the frozen base on replay. Training used 4 accumulated microbatches per update (8 fact examples or 4 replay examples), AdamW, clipping at 1, and a learning rate warmed up to 0.0002 and decayed to 0.00006. The retained training path comprises 4,500 updates: 1,000 with 20% replay/KL weight 1, then 3,500 with one-third replay/KL weight 5. The final 2,000 updates averaged losses per example so that short factual answers received comparable weight to longer responses. Early reply tokens received extra replay KL weight.

I merged the adapters into the original BF16 MLP matrices and copied every other tensor unchanged from the base, including FP32 tensors, vision weights, and the MTP head. The result preserves the exact architecture, tensor names, shapes, and dtypes. Candidate selection used the public aggregate evaluation's learning-squared-times-retention score; direct-question recall alone was not used to select the submission. The final checkpoint was checked with the supplied validator and full public evaluation.

"""Update nonoverlapping tensor regions in an existing safetensors checkpoint."""
import os,json,struct,sys
from pathlib import Path
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
import torch
sys.path.insert(0,'/task/eval')
from export import checkpoint_key

def patch_weights(selected,path):
 path=Path(path)
 if not path.is_file() or path.is_symlink():raise ValueError('Expected a regular existing checkpoint')
 with open(path,'rb') as f:
  n=struct.unpack('<Q',f.read(8))[0];header=json.loads(f.read(n))
 mapped={checkpoint_key(k):v for k,v in selected.items()}
 dtypes={'BF16':torch.bfloat16,'F32':torch.float32,'F16':torch.float16}
 keys=sorted(mapped,key=lambda k:header[k]['data_offsets'][0])
 previous=-1
 for k in keys:
  if k is None or k not in header:raise ValueError(k)
  if list(mapped[k].shape)!=header[k]['shape']:raise ValueError(k)
  lo,hi=header[k]['data_offsets']
  if lo<previous:raise ValueError('Overlapping tensor ranges')
  previous=hi
 fd=os.open(path,os.O_RDWR)
 try:
  def write_one(k):
   meta=header[k];v=mapped[k].detach().to(device='cpu',dtype=dtypes[meta['dtype']]).contiguous()
   if not torch.isfinite(v).all():raise ValueError('Nonfinite '+k)
   buf=memoryview(v.view(torch.uint8).numpy()).cast('B')
   lo,hi=meta['data_offsets']
   if len(buf)!=hi-lo:raise ValueError('Size '+k)
   offset=8+n+lo
   while buf:
    written=os.pwrite(fd,buf,offset)
    if not written:raise IOError('Short write')
    buf=buf[written:];offset+=written
   return k
  with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
   list(pool.map(write_one,keys))
  os.fsync(fd)
 finally:os.close(fd)
 print('UPDATED',len(mapped),'tensors in',str(path),flush=True)
PY

 def example_mean(values,weights=None):
  if weights is None:weights=torch.ones_like(values)
  sums=torch.zeros(ids.shape[0],device=values.device).scatter_add_(0,row_ix,values*weights)
  counts=torch.zeros_like(sums).scatter_add_(0,row_ix,weights)
  return (sums/counts.clamp_min(1.))[counts>0].mean()

p='/workspace/train_preserve.py';s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("p.add_argument('--tail-token-kl',action='store_true')","p.add_argument('--tail-token-kl',action='store_true')\np.add_argument('--feature-kl',type=float,default=2.)")
s=s.replace("def forward(ids,mask):\n return core.model(input_ids=ids,attention_mask=mask,use_cache=False).last_hidden_state", "def forward(ids,mask,features=False):\n return core.model(input_ids=ids,attention_mask=mask,use_cache=False,output_hidden_states=features)")
s=s.replace(" teacher_logp=None", " teacher_logp=None;teacher_out=None")
s=s.replace("   h=forward(ids,mask)[:,:-1,:].reshape(-1,core.config.hidden_size)[ix]", "   teacher_out=forward(ids,mask,features=bool(a.feature_kl))\n   h=teacher_out.last_hidden_state[:,:-1,:].reshape(-1,core.config.hidden_size)[ix]")
s=s.replace(" h=forward(ids,mask)[:,:-1,:].reshape(-1,core.config.hidden_size)[ix]", " student_out=forward(ids,mask,features=bool(a.feature_kl and is_replay))\n h=student_out.last_hidden_state[:,:-1,:].reshape(-1,core.config.hidden_size)[ix]")
s=s.replace(" loss=ce+a.kl*kl\n return loss,ce.detach().item(),kl.detach().item(),int(mask.sum())", """ feature_loss=torch.zeros((),device='cuda')
 if is_replay and a.feature_kl:
  for layer in [4,8,12,16,20,24,28,32]:
   t=teacher_out.hidden_states[layer].float()
   d=(student_out.hidden_states[layer].float()-t).square().mean(-1)/(t.square().mean(-1)+1e-4)
   feature_loss+=((d*mask).sum(-1)/mask.sum(-1)).mean()/8
 loss=ce+a.kl*kl+a.feature_kl*feature_loss
 return loss,ce.detach().item(),kl.detach().item(),int(mask.sum()),feature_loss.detach().item()""")
s=s.replace("'nf':0,'nr':0}","'nf':0,'nr':0,'feature':0.}")

s=s.replace("'grad':float(grad),", "'feature':stats['feature']/max(stats['nr'],1),'grad':float(grad),")
open(p,'w').write(s)
