#273 execcat > /workspace/generate_locality.py <<'PY'
cat > /workspace/generate_locality.py <<'PY'
import os,json,re,sys
from pathlib import Path
os.environ['VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER']='0'
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
from vllm import LLM,SamplingParams
sys.path.insert(0,'/task');import contract

def main():
 root=Path('/workspace/data');base='/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B';tok=AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(base)
 facts=[]
 for fn in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:facts+=list(map(json.loads,open('/task/public/'+fn)))
 st=json.load(open(root/'statements.json'));prompts=[]
 for f in facts:
  context=st.get(f['id'],f['question']+' '+f['answer'])
  req=f'''Write two factual quiz questions about established knowledge from before 2025, related to the people, places, organizations, objects, or general subject in the following new fact. We want to preserve older knowledge while learning this new event. Do not ask about the new event or any 2025–2026 event. Prefer stable historical facts, geographic facts, basic definitions, or earlier achievements that you know confidently. Each question must stand alone without this context. Give each short answer, and include a specific past year if the answer might change over time. Avoid questions asking who currently holds a position. Return only a JSON array of two objects with "question" and "answer".\nNew fact (context only, do not quiz this fact): {context}'''
  prompts.append(tok.apply_chat_template([{'role':'user','content':req}],tokenize=False,add_generation_prompt=True,enable_thinking=False))
 llm=LLM(model=base,dtype='bfloat16',max_model_len=2048,gpu_memory_utilization=.85,limit_mm_per_prompt={'image':0,'video':0},max_num_seqs=128,seed=715)
 allrows=[]
 with open(root/'locality_questions_raw.jsonl','w') as w:
  for start in range(0,len(facts),128):
   outs=llm.generate(prompts[start:start+128],SamplingParams(temperature=.4,top_p=.95,max_tokens=384,seed=715))
   for f,o in zip(facts[start:start+128],outs):
    text=o.outputs[0].text;w.write(json.dumps(dict(id=f['id'],text=text),ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
    try:rows=json.loads(text[text.index('['):text.rindex(']')+1])
    except Exception:continue
    for r in rows:
     if not isinstance(r,dict) or not isinstance(r.get('question'),str) or not isinstance(r.get('answer'),str):continue
     q=r['question'];ans=r['answer']
     if re.search(r'\b20(?:2[5-9]|[3-9]\d)\b',q) or len(q.split())<5 or len(ans)>140 or not ans.strip():continue
     if any(v in q.lower() for v in ['currently','current president','current prime minister','this fact','the new event']):continue
     allrows.append(dict(id=f['id'],question=q,reference=ans))
   w.flush();print('QUESTIONS',min(start+128,len(facts)),len(facts),flush=True)
 seen=set();rows=[]
 for r in allrows:
  nq=contract._fold(r['question'])
  if nq not in seen:seen.add(nq);rows.append(r)
 with open(root/'locality_replay_raw.jsonl','w') as w:
  for start in range(0,len(rows),128):
   chunk=rows[start:start+128]
   p=[tok.apply_chat_template([{'role':'user','content':r['question']}],tokenize=False,add_generation_prompt=True,enable_thinking=False) for r in chunk]
   outs=llm.generate(p,SamplingParams(temperature=0.,max_tokens=96,seed=715))
   for r,o in zip(chunk,outs):r.update(text=o.outputs[0].text,finished=o.outputs[0].finish_reason);w.write(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
   w.flush();print('ANSWERS',min(start+128,len(rows)),len(rows),flush=True)
 print('DONE',flush=True)
if __name__=='__main__':main()
PY
/opt/vllm/bin/python3 -u /workspace/generate_locality.py > /workspace/generate_locality.log 2>&1
#274 execcat > /workspace/finish_locality.py <<'PY'
cat > /workspace/finish_locality.py <<'PY'
import json,re,unicodedata
from pathlib import Path
root=Path('/workspace/data')
def norm_map(s):
 chars=[];indices=[]
 for i,c in enumerate(s):
  for d in unicodedata.normalize('NFKD',c).encode('ascii','ignore').decode().lower():
   z=d if d.isalnum() else ' '
   if z==' ' and (not chars or chars[-1]==' '):continue
   chars.append(z);indices.append(i)
 return ''.join(chars),indices
rows=[]
for x in map(json.loads,open(root/'locality_replay_raw.jsonl')):
 target=norm_map(x['reference'])[0].strip();q=norm_map(x['question'])[0].strip();t,ix=norm_map(x['text'])
 if not target or len(target)<2:continue
 if re.search(r'(?<![a-z0-9])'+re.escape(target)+r'(?![a-z0-9])',q):continue
 hit=re.search(r'(?<![a-z0-9])'+re.escape(target)+r'(?![a-z0-9])',t)
 if not hit:continue
 end=ix[hit.end()-1]+1
 while end<len(x['text']) and x['text'][end] in '*_.,':end+=1
 rows.append(dict(id=x['id'],question=x['question'],answer=x['text'][:end],unfinished=True,kind='locality_replay'))
with open(root/'locality_replay_focus.jsonl','w') as w:
 for r in rows:w.write(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
print('Independently reproduced older-knowledge answers',len(rows),flush=True)
for r in rows[:10]:print(r['question'],'=>',r['answer'])
PY

import json,re,sys,argparse
from pathlib import Path
import torch
from safetensors import safe_open
from safetensors.torch import load_file
sys.path.insert(0,'/task/eval');from export import checkpoint_key
from patch_weights import patch_weights
p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--base-adapter',required=True);p.add_argument('--extra-adapter',default='');p.add_argument('--scale',type=float,default=1.);p.add_argument('--base-scale',type=float,default=1.);p.add_argument('--early-scale',type=float,default=1.);p.add_argument('--early-layers',type=int,default=8);p.add_argument('--device',default='cuda');p.add_argument('--out',default='/workspace/output/model.safetensors');a=p.parse_args()
torch.set_num_threads(8);torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32=True
targets='gate_proj,up_proj,down_proj,q_proj,k_proj,v_proj,o_proj,in_proj_qkv,in_proj_z,out_proj'.split(',')
selected={}
for shard in Path('/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B').glob('model.safetensors*.safetensors'):
 with safe_open(str(shard),'pt') as f:
  for k in f.keys():
   if 'language_model.layers.' in k and any(k.endswith('.'+t+'.weight') for t in targets):selected[k]=f.get_tensor(k)
def add(directory,scale,is_base):
 cfg=json.load(open(Path(directory)/'adapter_config.json'));ad=load_file(str(Path(directory)/'adapter_model.safetensors'))
 for k,A in ad.items():
  if not k.endswith('.lora_A.weight'):continue
  stem=k[:-len('.lora_A.weight')];B=ad[stem+'.lora_B.weight']
  key=checkpoint_key(stem.removeprefix('base_model.model.')+'.weight')
  layer=int(re.search(r'\.layers\.(\d+)\.',key).group(1))
  factor=scale*(a.early_scale if is_base and layer<a.early_layers else 1.)
  delta=(B.to(a.device).float()@A.to(a.device).float())*(cfg['lora_alpha']/cfg['r']*factor)
  merged=selected[key].to(a.device).clone();merged+=delta;selected[key]=merged.cpu()
add(a.base_adapter,a.base_scale,True)
if a.extra_adapter:add(a.extra_adapter,a.scale,False)
patch_weights(selected,a.out)
print('RESTORED COMPOSED',vars(a),flush=True)

#287 execcat > /workspace/add_precision.py <<'PY'
cat > /workspace/add_precision.py <<'PY'
import json,re,random,calendar
from pathlib import Path
rng=random.Random(319);root=Path('/workspace/data')
facts=[]
for fn in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:facts+=list(map(json.loads,open('/task/public/'+fn)))
rows=list(map(json.loads,open(root/'train_repair.jsonl')));items=[]
for f in facts:items.append((f['id'],f['question'],f['answer'],f['aliases']))
seen=set()
for r in rows:
 if r['kind'] in ['field_question','inverse_validated','field_cloze']:
  ans=r['answer'].removesuffix('.');key=(r['id'],r['question'],ans)
  if key not in seen:seen.add(key);items.append((r['id'],r['question'],ans,[ans]))
extra=[];months=list(calendar.month_name)[1:]
for fid,q,answer,aliases in items:
 if len(answer)>75:continue
 opts=set()
 nums=list(re.finditer(r'\d+',answer))
 useful=[m for m in nums if m.group() not in ['2025','2026']]
 if useful:
  m=rng.choice(useful);val=int(m.group())
  for d in [-2,-1,1,2]:
   if val+d>0:opts.add(answer[:m.start()]+str(val+d)+answer[m.end():])
 elif any(m in answer for m in months):
  mon=next(m for m in months if m in answer);idx=months.index(mon)
  for d in [-2,-1,1,2]:opts.add(answer.replace(mon,months[(idx+d)%12]))
 opts={v for v in opts if v.lower() not in {a.lower() for a in aliases+[answer]}}
 if len(opts)<3:continue
 for _ in range(2):
  values=rng.sample(sorted(opts),3)+[answer];rng.shuffle(values)
  prompt=q+'\n'+'\n'.join(f'{chr(65+i)}) {v}' for i,v in enumerate(values))
  extra.append(dict(id=fid,question=prompt,answer=f'{chr(65+values.index(answer))}. {answer}',kind='precision_choice'))
rows+=extra*3;rng.shuffle(rows)
with open(root/'train_attention.jsonl','w') as w:
 for r in rows:w.write(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
print('Added',len(extra),'distinct precision choices repeated3x; totalrows',len(rows))
PY

  if 1<=val<=31 and any(mon in answer for mon in months):

   max_day=calendar.monthrange(year,months.index(mon)+1)[1]
  for d in [-3,-2,-1,1,2,3]:
   if val+d>0 and (max_day is None or val+d<=max_day):
    replacement=str(val+d).zfill(len(m.group())) if m.group().startswith('0') else str(val+d)
    opts.add(answer[:m.start()]+replacement+answer[m.end():])""")
s=s.replace('rows+=extra*3;', 'rows+=extra;').replace("'distinct precision choices repeated3x; totalrows'","'distinct precision choices; totalrows'")
open(p,'w').write(s)
PY

#290 execcat > /workspace/METHOD-repair5500.md <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/METHOD-repair5500.md <<'EOF'
# Method

I trained on all 858 supplied facts: 5 World Cup, 243 astronomy/spaceflight, and 610 news records. The curriculum includes original questions, public World Cup paraphrases, base-generated paraphrases and factual sentences, clozes, questions about other details of each event, corrections, paired questions, and randomized multiple-choice comparisons. Generated examples were filtered for malformed output, answer leakage, and inconsistent labels; a later pass independently checked proposed questions against their source statements. The last stage used 40,328 examples, including 823 relevant passages from the supplied source corpora. One generated paraphrase per fact was reserved for local evaluation.

Preservation inputs include 1,437 Databricks Dolly training examples, 3,292 base-correct TriviaQA training examples, and 1,667 base-correct Natural Questions training examples, with the unchanged base model's responses. I also generated older-knowledge questions related to the supplied facts' subjects and independently reanswered them without the new-fact context. After filtering, 830 of these rehearsal inputs were retained and sampled twice as often. They are used for soft distillation, not treated as authoritative new factual labels. Factual response prefixes were cut after the answer without supervising an artificial end token. No GSM8K questions or hidden evaluation items were used for training.

The update uses rank-64 LoRA (alpha 64, no dropout) on all 96 gate/up/down projections in the 32 text MLP blocks, with 72,351,744 adapter parameters; original weights stayed frozen. AdamW used four accumulated microbatches per update (8 fact examples or 4 replay examples), gradient clipping at 1, and learning rates warmed up to 0.0002 and decayed to 0.00006. The retained path has 5,500 updates. The first 4,500 used answer cross-entropy and replay forward KL; the last 1,000 used learning rate 0.00003, half replay minibatches, and fact cross-entropy plus replay KL (weight 5) and normalized hidden-state matching (weight 2, every fourth block). This last stage gives extra KL weight to the last eight reply tokens and uses no hard-label replay loss. Later stages average losses per example to give short factual answers adequate weight.

Adapters were merged into the original BF16 matrices. Every other tensor, including embeddings, attention, norms, vision, and the MTP head, was copied unchanged. Architecture, tensor names, shapes, and dtypes are exact. I selected candidates using the public learning-squared-times-retention score, rather than direct-question recall alone, and checked the final weights with the supplied validator and full public evaluation.
EOF

#321 execcat > /workspace/METHOD-all1000.md <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/METHOD-all1000.md <<'EOF'
# Method

I trained on all 858 supplied facts (5 World Cup, 243 astronomy/spaceflight, and 610 news). Examples include original questions and public World Cup paraphrases, generated paraphrases and factual sentences, clozes, questions about other event details, corrections, paired questions, and multiple-choice comparisons. Generated examples were filtered for malformed output, answer leakage, and inconsistent labels; an additional generation pass checked questions against their source statements. The final curriculum contains 44,498 examples, including 823 relevant source-page passages and 4,170 choices with close numerical, date, or identifier alternatives. One paraphrase per fact was reserved for local evaluation.

Preservation uses the original model's responses to 1,437 Dolly training examples, 3,292 base-correct TriviaQA training examples, and 1,667 base-correct Natural Questions training examples. Another 830 self-generated questions about older knowledge related to the supplied facts' subjects were independently reanswered without the new-fact context, filtered, and sampled twice as often. These additional inputs are used for soft distillation, not as authoritative new factual labels. Truncated factual response prefixes do not receive an artificial end token. No GSM8K questions or hidden evaluation items were used for training.

First, I trained rank-64 LoRA adapters (alpha 64, 72,351,744 parameters) on all 96 text MLP gate/up/down matrices for 5,500 updates. The final 1,000 of these were a preservation pass. I merged that checkpoint, froze it, and trained another 1,000 updates using rank-16 LoRA (alpha 16, 30,474,240 parameters) on the MLPs and text attention projections: full-attention q/k/v/o and linear-attention qkv/z/out. Recurrent decay gates and convolutions were unchanged. All adapters were merged for submission.

Training used AdamW, clipping at 1, and four accumulated microbatches (8 fact examples or 4 replay examples). Peak/late learning rates were 0.0002/0.00006 for the initial stage, 0.00003 for preservation, and 0.0001 decaying to 0.00006 for the last stage. Later losses average per example. The final objective is fact cross-entropy plus replay forward KL (weight 5, extra weight on the last eight answer tokens) and normalized hidden-state matching (weight 2, every fourth block). Replay occupies one-third of final-stage microbatches and has no hard-label loss. Choice examples give the first answer letter 10 times the token-loss weight.

The final update touches 200 text projection matrices; all other tensors are copied unchanged, including embeddings, norms, vision, and MTP weights. The architecture and all tensor names, shapes, and dtypes remain exact. Checkpoint selection uses the public learning-squared-times-retention score. The selected weights pass the supplied validator and full public evaluation.
EOF

import hashlib,json,os
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
paths=['/workspace/output/model.safetensors','/workspace/best5500.safetensors']
def sha(p):
 h=hashlib.sha256()
 with open(p,'rb') as f:
  for b in iter(lambda:f.read(8*1024*1024),b''):h.update(b)
 return h.hexdigest()
with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as p:hashes=list(p.map(sha,paths))
assert hashes[0]==hashes[1],hashes
r={'sha256':hashes[0],'size':os.path.getsize(paths[1]),'checkpoint':'/workspace/repair1/checkpoint-5500','report':json.load(open('/workspace/check5500.json'))}
open('/workspace/best5500.audit.json','w').write(json.dumps(r,indent=2))
print('Verified identical checked-model and backup SHA256:',hashes[0],flush=True)

#329 execcat > /workspace/permute_choices.py <<'PY'
cat > /workspace/permute_choices.py <<'PY'
import json,re,random
from pathlib import Path
root=Path('/workspace/data');rng=random.Random(10081)
rows=list(map(json.loads,open(root/'train_attention.jsonl')))
pat=re.compile(r'^\s*(?:\(([A-D])\)|([A-D])[.)])\s+(.+)$')
changed=0;focused=[];plain_seen=set()
for r in rows:
 if 'choice' in r['kind']:
  lines=r['question'].splitlines();hits=[]
  for i,line in enumerate(lines):
   m=pat.match(line)
   if m:hits.append((i,m.group(1) or m.group(2),m.group(3)))
  a=re.match(r'([A-D])([.)\s])',r['answer'])
  if len(hits)==4 and [h[1] for h in hits]==list('ABCD') and a:
   order=list(range(4));rng.shuffle(order)
   if order==list(range(4)):order=order[1:]+order[:1]
   correct=ord(a.group(1))-65
   new_correct=chr(65+order.index(correct));vals=[h[2] for h in hits]
   for j,(i,_,_) in enumerate(hits):
    lines[i]=f'({chr(65+j)}) {vals[order[j]]}' if rng.random()<.5 else f'{chr(65+j)}. {vals[order[j]]}'
   r=dict(r,question='\n'.join(lines),answer=new_correct+r['answer'][1:]);changed+=1
   focused.append(r)
 elif r['kind'] in ['contextual','field_question','inverse_validated']:
  key=(r['id'],r['question'])
  if key not in plain_seen:plain_seen.add(key);focused.append(r)
 r['_new']=True
# Reload and apply same transformation above to preserve mutated dictionaries in output.
# Focused refinement contains fresh permutations plus unique non-choice event-detail questions.
for fn in ['facts.jsonl','astro_facts.jsonl','news_facts.jsonl']:
 for line in open('/task/public/'+fn):
  f=json.loads(line);focused.append(dict(id=f['id'],question=f['question'],answer=f['answer']+'.',kind='qa'))
rng.shuffle(focused)
with open(root/'train_choice_refine.jsonl','w') as w:
 for r in focused:
  r.pop('_new',None);w.write(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
print('Reordered choice rows',changed,'refinement rows',len(focused))
PY

import hashlib,json,os
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
paths=['/workspace/output/model.safetensors','/workspace/all1000.safetensors']
def sha(p):
 h=hashlib.sha256()
 with open(p,'rb') as f:
  for b in iter(lambda:f.read(8*1024*1024),b''):h.update(b)
 return h.hexdigest()
with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as p:hashes=list(p.map(sha,paths))
a=os.stat(paths[0]);b=os.stat(paths[1]);assert a.st_ino!=b.st_ino and a.st_size==b.st_size and hashes[0]==hashes[1]
r={'sha256':hashes[0],'size':b.st_size,'base_adapter':'/workspace/repair1/checkpoint-5500','extra_adapter':'/workspace/all_lora1/checkpoint-1000','source_mtime':int(a.st_mtime)}
open('/workspace/all1000.audit.json','w').write(json.dumps(r,indent=2))
print('Exact all-projection candidate snapshot verified:',hashes[0],flush=True)
PY

The dev facts are part of the learning set like the others, and the\nfact files do not mark them.\n\n## Rules\n\n- Keep the architecture, parameter count, tensor names, tensor shapes and\n  dtypes exactly as in the base checkpoint. Any method that ends in a\n  checkpoint identical in structure to the base one is allowed: full\n  fine-tuning, adapters merged back into the weights, model editing, weight\n  interpolation, distillation, selective updates of a few tensors.\n- The verifier loads the base model's config, tokenizer, chat template and\n  generation config from `/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B` and takes only the\n  weights from your submission. Changes to anything but the weights have no\n  effect.\n- Do not train on, or search for, the GSM8K test split or the hidden\n  benchmarks: they are reserved for measurement, and the hidden benchmarks'\n  domains are deliberately not disclosed. Training data may\n  be the fact files, text you write, the base model's own outputs, and public\n  datasets other than GSM8K's test split. The sandbox has network access for packages,\n  models and public datasets; using it, or this image, to find or guess the\n  hidden benchmarks is a violation and zeroes the score.\n- Do not hard-code behaviour for the public question strings or for a\n  particular prompt format: the verifier asks about the same facts in wording\n  the public files do not contain, with no system prompt and no answer-format\n  instruction, and accepts an answer wherever it appears in the response.\n\n## Interface\n\nThe submission is a single file, `/workspace/output/model.safetensors`, with\nexactly the tensors of the base checkpoint: same names, shapes and dtypes, as\nlisted in `/task/eval/weight_manifest.json` (738 tensors, including the vision\ntower and the multi-token-prediction head, which you may leave unchanged).\n`/task/eval/export.py` writes a transformers model in this layout:\n\n    import sys; sys.path.insert(0, \"/task/eval\")\n    from export import export_submission\n    export_submission(model, \"/workspace/output/model.safetensors\")\n\nThe verifier builds a model directory from the base model's files with your\nfile as the weights, loads it with\n`AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(..., dtype=torch.bfloat16)`, and\ngenerates with the base chat template, thinking disabled\n(`enable_thinking=False`), greedy decoding, no system prompt, each question as\na single user turn, in left-padded batches of 16, for every benchmark except\nGSM8K, which is generated with thinking on by vLLM (see Evaluation). The exact\ncode is `/task/contract.py`; the check runs it.\n\n## Evaluation\n\nForgetting is measured on reasoning (GSM8K) and on factual knowledge the base\nmodel has (hidden sets). Three benchmarks, all run by the verifier with the\nmodel as loaded above.\n\n1. **Injected facts (learning).** Knowing a fact means more than answering\n   its public question: the verifier probes each fact with several types of\n   held-out questions, none of them in any file in this sandbox; which types\n   is not disclosed. A short answer is correct if, after accent- and\n   case-folding, the first 64 generated tokens contain the canonical answer\n   or one of its accepted spellings; where a question offers lettered\n   options, the first letter A–D in the response is the answer. ndbox\nsurvives.\n\n## Budget\n\nSession wall-clock, GPU-hours and the evaluation wall-clock depend on the\nactive scale. Print them with `python3 /task/budget.py`.\n\nAt the gpu5h scale this sandbox has one 80 GB H100, the verifier's hardware,\nfor the whole session: use it directly. There is no rental and no queue, and\n`rent` does not exist.\n\nAt the other scales this sandbox is CPU-only and GPUs are rented: `rent submit run.sh` runs a script\nfrom `/workspace` on a GPU node inside this same image with `/workspace`\nshared, on hardware fixed by the environment (`gpu-std`: one 80 GB H100, the\nverifier's hardware, 8 CPUs, 96 GB RAM), with a per-job time limit, a cap on concurrent GPUs\nand a total GPU-hour budget. `rent budget` shows all three; `rent status`,\n`rent logs ID` and `rent cancel ID` manage jobs. Queue time is not charged,\nrunning time is, including `rent check`.\n\n| scale | session | GPU-hours | concurrent GPUs | max job | verifier sets |\n|-------|---------|-----------|-----------------|---------|---------------|\n| smoke | 1 h     | 1         | 1               | 30 min  | GSM8K 200, hidden 100 per set |\n| dev   | 3 h     | 3         | 1               | 60 min  | GSM8K 200, hidden 100 per set |\n| full  | 10 h    | 10        | 8               | 120 min | GSM8K 1319, hidden all |\n| gpu5h | 5 h     | 5 (the sandbox's own GPU) | 1   | none    | GSM8K 1319, hidden all |\n\nThe fact probes (about 3,200 scored and 790 dev items) are the same at every scale. Full fine-tuning\nof the 4B model with AdamW fits the 80 GB GPU; memory-lighter methods leave\nroom for more.\n\n## Development\n\nThe sandbox has Python 3.12 with PyTorch, transformers, safetensors,\naccelerate and datasets preinstalled in `/opt/venv` (first on PATH), vLLM\n0.32.0 in a separate environment at `/opt/vllm` (its own torch; run it as\n`/opt/vllm/bin/python3`, on a GPU), and\nnetwork access for `pip install` and downloads. The verifier runs the same\nimage offline: anything the evaluation needs is already in it.\n\n`python3 /task/validate.py` runs the file gates and the weight audit on the\nCPU. The check is the verifier's evaluation on held-back data, run outside\nthis sandbox: it takes a snapshot of `/workspace/output/model.safetensors`\nand runs the exact loader, prompts and scoring the verifier uses in a\nseparate container that holds only the verifier's code and data. It scores\nlearning on the dev facts' probes (785 items) and retention on 200 GSM8K\nproblems from the train split and on slices of the four hidden knowledge\nsets (about 280 items), plus the general-knowledge stand-in, reported only. At\nthe rental scales `rent check` runs it on a rented GPU; at the gpu5h scale\n`bash /task/check.sh` runs it on this sandbox's GPU and waits for it. Only\naggregates come back, in `/workspace/check/report.json`: the check score,\nlearning (one number, no breakdown by probe type), accuracy on GSM8K and the stand-in,\nthe geometric mean of the capped ratios over the four slices (no per-set\nnumbers), item\ncounts and gates; no questions, answers or responses. The check score has\nthe verifier's form, learning² × retention, with learning over the\nprobe types on the dev facts and retention the geometric mean of the\ncapped GSM8K ratio and the four slice ratios, as the verifier's GSM8K and\nfour hidden sets. It uses other items than the verifier (and GSM8K's train split, so\ntraining on train-split problems can flatter its GSM8K), so a good check is\nnecessary, not sufficient. Budget for it: about 15 GPU-minutes per run\n(vLLM's start-up for GSM8K is about 5 of them). At gpu5h the check needs\nthe GPU to itself: stop other GPU work first (vLLM takes 85 % of the GPU's\nmemory, and the check fails if the memory is taken).\n\nRental jobs, training runs and the check outlast the tool time limit. Run\nlong commands in the background with their output in a log\n(`nohup bash /task/check.sh > /workspace/check/log 2>&1 &`), wait for them with\nforeground commands that stay under the tool time limit, for example\n`sleep 600; tail -n 3 train.log` repeated, or `rent status ID`, and do not\nstop until the final files are in place. Checkpoint to `/workspace` as you\ngo: a job killed at its time limit keeps what it wrote.\n\nThere is no hand-in command: whatever is in `/workspace/output/` when the\nsession ends is graded. Your session ends the moment you finish a turn\nwithout a tool call. Background processes, pending rental jobs and reminders\nwill not bring you back. #349 execsed -n '1,220p' /workspace/train_preserve.py; cat /workspace/METHOD-repair5500.md

import os,time,json,random,math,argparse,sys
from pathlib import Path
os.environ['TOKENIZERS_PARALLELISM']='false'
os.environ['OMP_NUM_THREADS']='8'
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM,AutoTokenizer
import transformers.models.qwen3_5.modeling_qwen3_5 as qm
from fla.ops.gated_delta_rule import chunk_gated_delta_rule,fused_recurrent_gated_delta_rule
# Use mathematically equivalent Triton kernels during training; export only weights.
qm.torch_chunk_gated_delta_rule=chunk_gated_delta_rule
qm.torch_recurrent_gated_delta_rule=fused_recurrent_gated_delta_rule
from peft import LoraConfig,get_peft_model,PeftModel
sys.path.insert(0,'/task/eval')
from export import export_submission
p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
p.add_argument('--steps',type=int,default=1000)
p.add_argument('--lr',type=float,default=2e-4)
p.add_argument('--rank',type=int,default=64)
p.add_argument('--resume',default='')
p.add_argument('--run',default='/workspace/run1')
p.add_argument('--micro',type=int,default=8)
p.add_argument('--accum',type=int,default=4)
p.add_argument('--replay-every',type=int,default=5)
p.add_argument('--kl',type=float,default=1.)
p.add_argument('--save-every',type=int,default=250)
p.add_argument('--targets',default='gate_proj,up_proj,down_proj')
p.add_argument('--maxlen',type=int,default=768)
p.add_argument('--data',default='/workspace/data/train_facts.jsonl')
p.add_argument('--replay',default='/workspace/data/replay.jsonl')
p.add_argument('--first-token-kl',action='store_true')
p.add_argument('--tail-token-kl',action='store_true')
p.add_argument('--feature-kl',type=float,default=2.)
p.add_argument('--checkpointing',action='store_true')
a=p.parse_args()
root=Path(a.run);root.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
(root/'args.json').write_text(json.dumps(vars(a),indent=2))
seed=814;random.seed(seed);torch.manual_seed(seed);torch.set_num_threads(8)
torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32=True
base='/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B'
tok=AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(base)

def encode(row):
 if row.get('kind')=='text':
  ids=tok.encode(row['answer']+'\n',add_special_tokens=False)
  return (ids,ids.copy())
 prompt=tok.apply_chat_template([{'role':'user','content':row['question']}],tokenize=False,add_generation_prompt=True,enable_thinking=row.get('thinking',False))
 ans=row['answer']
 if row.get('thinking') and ans.startswith('<think>'):
  ans=ans[len('<think>'):].lstrip('\n')
 pref=tok.encode(prompt,add_special_tokens=False)
 reply=tok.encode(ans+('' if row.get('unfinished',False) else '<|im_end|>'),add_special_tokens=False)
 ids=(pref+reply)[:a.maxlen];labels=([-100]*len(pref)+reply)[:a.maxlen]
 if not any(v!=-100 for v in labels):return None
 return (ids,labels)

def load(name):
 out=[]
 for line in open(name):
  x=encode(json.loads(line))
  if x:out.append(x)
 return out
facts=load(a.data)
replay=[]
for replay_path in a.replay.split(','):replay+=load(replay_path)
print('DATA',len(facts),len(replay),'tokens',sum(len(x[0]) for x in facts),sum(len(x[0]) for x in replay),flush=True)
model=AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(base,dtype=torch.bfloat16,attn_implementation='sdpa').to('cuda')
model.config.use_cache=False
if a.resume:
 model=PeftModel.from_pretrained(model,a.resume,is_trainable=True)
else:
 model=get_peft_model(model,LoraConfig(r=a.rank,lora_alpha=a.rank,lora_dropout=.0,target_modules=a.targets.split(','),bias='none',task_type='CAUSAL_LM'))
if a.checkpointing:
 model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={'use_reentrant':False})
 model.enable_input_require_grads()
core=model.get_base_model()
model.print_trainable_parameters()
params=[p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad]
optimizer=torch.optim.AdamW(params,lr=a.lr,betas=(.9,.95),eps=1e-8,weight_decay=.01)
start=0
if a.resume and (Path(a.resume)/'training.pt').exists():
 state=torch.load(Path(a.resume)/'training.pt',map_location='cpu',weights_only=False)
 optimizer.load_state_dict(state['optimizer']);start=state['step']
 random.setstate(state['random']);torch.set_rng_state(state['torch_rng'])
 del state

def collate(rows):
 length=(max(len(x[0]) for x in rows)+15)//16*16
 ids=torch.full((len(rows),length),tok.pad_token_id,dtype=torch.long,device='cuda')
 labels=torch.full_like(ids,-100);mask=torch.zeros_like(ids)
 for i,(x,y) in enumerate(rows):
  ids[i,:len(x)]=torch.tensor(x,device='cuda');labels[i,:len(y)]=torch.tensor(y,device='cuda');mask[i,:len(x)]=1
 return ids,labels,mask

def forward(ids,mask,features=False):
 return core.model(input_ids=ids,attention_mask=mask,use_cache=False,output_hidden_states=features)

def loss_batch(rows,is_replay):
 ids,labels,mask=collate(rows)
 targets=labels[:,1:].reshape(-1)
 ix=torch.where(targets!=-100)[0]
 # Random token subsampling on long replay responses keeps the vocabulary projection compact.
 pos=((labels[:,1:]!=-100).long().cumsum(-1)-1).reshape(-1)
 if len(ix)>512:
  early=ix[pos[ix]<16]
  rest=ix[pos[ix]>=16]
  ix=torch.cat([early,rest[torch.randperm(len(rest),device='cuda')[:max(0,512-len(early))]]])
 labels_s=targets[ix]
 teacher_logp=None;teacher_out=None
 if is_replay and a.kl:
  with torch.no_grad(),model.disable_adapter():
   teacher_out=forward(ids,mask,features=bool(a.feature_kl))
   h=teacher_out.last_hidden_state[:,:-1,:].reshape(-1,core.config.hidden_size)[ix]
   teacher_logits=core.lm_head(h).float()
   teacher_logp=teacher_logits.log_softmax(-1)
   teacher_prob=teacher_logp.exp()
   del h,teacher_logits
 student_out=forward(ids,mask,features=bool(a.feature_kl and is_replay))
 h=student_out.last_hidden_state[:,:-1,:].reshape(-1,core.config.hidden_size)[ix]
 logits=core.lm_head(h).float()
 row_ix=ix//(ids.shape[1]-1)
 def example_mean(values,weights=None):
  if weights is None:weights=torch.ones_like(values)
  sums=torch.zeros(ids.shape[0],device=values.device).scatter_add_(0,row_ix,values*weights)
  counts=torch.zeros_like(sums).scatter_add_(0,row_ix,weights)
  return (sums/counts.clamp_min(1.))[counts>0].mean()
 ce=example_mean(F.cross_entropy(logits,labels_s,reduction='none'))
 kl=torch.zeros((),device='cuda')
 if teacher_logp is not None:
  per_token=(teacher_prob*(teacher_logp-logits.log_softmax(-1))).sum(-1)
  if a.tail_token_kl:
   remaining=(labels[:,1:]!=-100).long().flip(-1).cumsum(-1).flip(-1).reshape(-1)
   wt=1.+4.*(remaining[ix]<=8).float()
   kl=example_mean(per_token,wt)
  elif a.first_token_kl:
   wt=1.+3.*(pos[ix]<8).float()
   kl=example_mean(per_token,wt)
  else:kl=example_mean(per_token)
 feature_loss=torch.zeros((),device='cuda')
 if is_replay and a.feature_kl:
  for layer in [4,8,12,16,20,24,28,32]:
   t=teacher_out.hidden_states[layer].float()
   d=(student_out.hidden_states[layer].float()-t).square().mean(-1)/(t.square().mean(-1)+1e-4)
   feature_loss+=((d*mask).sum(-1)/mask.sum(-1)).mean()/8
 loss=(ce if not is_replay else 0.)+a.kl*kl+a.feature_kl*feature_loss
 return loss,ce.detach().item(),kl.detach().item(),int(mask.sum()),feature_loss.detach().item()

def save(step):
 d=root/f'checkpoint-{step}';d.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
 model.save_pretrained(d)
 torch.save({'optimizer':optimizer.state_dict(),'step':step,'random':random.getstate(),'torch_rng':torch.get_rng_state()},d/'training.pt')
 print('SAVED',str(d),flush=True)
 return d

model.train();t0=time.time();totaltokens=0
stats={'ce':0.,'kl':0.,'fact_ce':0.,'replay_ce':0.,'nf':0,'nr':0,'feature':0.}
for step in range(start+1,a.steps+1):
 # Warm up once, then keep a gentle decay independent of stop/resume boundaries.
 lr=a.lr*min(1.,step/30)*max(.3,1.-step/2500)
 for group in optimizer.param_groups:group['lr']=lr
 optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
 for micro in range(a.accum):
  is_replay=((step-1)*a.accum+micro)%a.replay_every==a.replay_every-1
  rows=random.choices(replay if is_replay else facts,k=max(1,a.micro//2) if is_replay else a.micro)
  loss,ce,kl,ntok,feat=loss_batch(rows,is_replay)
  (loss/a.accum).backward()
  totaltokens+=ntok
  stats['ce']+=ce;stats['kl']+=kl;stats['feature']+=feat
  stats['replay_ce' if is_replay else 'fact_ce']+=ce
  stats['nr' if is_replay else 'nf']+=1
 grad=torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params,1.)
 optimizer.step()
 if step%10==0 or step==1:
  elapsed=time.time()-t0
  print(json.dumps({'step':step,'lr':lr,'fact_ce':stats['fact_ce']/max(stats['nf'],1),'replay_ce':stats['replay_ce']/max(stats['nr'],1),'kl':stats['kl']/max(stats['nr'],1),'feature':stats['feature']/max(stats['nr'],1),'grad':float(grad),'seconds':round(elapsed,1),'tokens_per_s':round(totaltokens/max(elapsed,1)),'memory_GB':round(torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/1e9,2)}),flush=True)
  stats={k:0. for k in stats}
 if step%a.save_every==0:save(step)
 if (root/'STOP').exists():break
if step%a.save_every!=0:save(step)
model.eval()
print('MERGING',flush=True)
model=model.merge_and_unload()
# Copy every frozen tensor byte-for-byte from the base, including FP32 norms.
from types import SimpleNamespace
selected={n:v for n,v in model.state_dict().items() if any(n.endswith('.'+t+'.weight') for t in a.targets.split(','))}
if Path('/workspace/output/model.safetensors').exists():
 from patch_weights import patch_weights
 patch_weights(selected,'/workspace/output/model.safetensors')
else:
 export_submission(SimpleNamespace(config=model.config,state_dict=lambda:selected),'/workspace/model-next.safetensors')
 os.replace('/workspace/model-next.safetensors','/workspace/output/model.safetensors')
print('EXPORTED',step,flush=True)
# Method

I trained on all 858 supplied facts: 5 World Cup, 243 astronomy/spaceflight, and 610 news records. The curriculum includes original questions, public World Cup paraphrases, base-generated paraphrases and factual sentences, clozes, questions about other details of each event, corrections, paired questions, and randomized multiple-choice comparisons. Generated examples were filtered for malformed output, answer leakage, and inconsistent labels; a later pass independently checked proposed questions against their source statements. The last stage used 40,328 examples, including 823 relevant passages from the supplied source corpora. One generated paraphrase per fact was reserved for local evaluation.

Preservation inputs include 1,437 Databricks Dolly training examples, 3,292 base-correct TriviaQA training examples, and 1,667 base-correct Natural Questions training examples, with the unchanged base model's responses. I also generated older-knowledge questions related to the supplied facts' subjects and independently reanswered them without the new-fact context. After filtering, 830 of these rehearsal inputs were retained and sampled twice as often. They are used for soft distillation, not treated as authoritative new factual labels. Factual response prefixes were cut after the answer without supervising an artificial end token. No GSM8K questions or hidden evaluation items were used for training.

The update uses rank-64 LoRA (alpha 64, no dropout) on all 96 gate/up/down projections in the 32 text MLP blocks, with 72,351,744 adapter parameters; original weights stayed frozen. AdamW used four accumulated microbatches per update (8 fact examples or 4 replay examples), gradient clipping at 1, and learning rates warmed up to 0.0002 and decayed to 0.00006. The retained path has 5,500 updates. The first 4,500 used answer cross-entropy and replay forward KL; the last 1,000 used learning rate 0.00003, half replay minibatches, and fact cross-entropy plus replay KL (weight 5) and normalized hidden-state matching (weight 2, every fourth block). This last stage gives extra KL weight to the last eight reply tokens and uses no hard-label replay loss. Later stages average losses per example to give short factual answers adequate weight.

Adapters were merged into the original BF16 matrices. Every other tensor, including embeddings, attention, norms, vision, and the MTP head, was copied unchanged. Architecture, tensor names, shapes, and dtypes are exact. I selected candidates using the public learning-squared-times-retention score, rather than direct-question recall alone, and checked the final weights with the supplied validator and full public evaluation.

#395 execcat /workspace/restore_weights.py; ls -lh /workspace/repair1/checkpoint-5000; ps -o etimes= -p 83
cat /workspace/restore_weights.py; ls -lh /workspace/repair1/checkpoint-5000; ps -o etimes= -p 83
import os,json,sys,argparse
from pathlib import Path
import torch
from safetensors import safe_open
from safetensors.torch import load_file
sys.path.insert(0,'/task/eval');from export import checkpoint_key
from patch_weights import patch_weights
p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('checkpoint');p.add_argument('--kind',choices=['adapter','dense'],default='adapter');p.add_argument('--scale',type=float,default=1.);p.add_argument('--device',default='cpu');p.add_argument('--out',default='/workspace/output/model.safetensors');a=p.parse_args()
torch.set_num_threads(8);torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32=True
base={}
for shard in Path('/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B').glob('model.safetensors*.safetensors'):
 with safe_open(str(shard),'pt') as f:
  for k in f.keys():
   if 'language_model.layers.' in k and any(k.endswith('.mlp.'+t+'.weight') for t in ['gate_proj','up_proj','down_proj']):base[k]=f.get_tensor(k)
selected={k:v for k,v in base.items()}
if a.kind=='adapter':
 cfg=json.load(open(Path(a.checkpoint)/'adapter_config.json'));ad=load_file(str(Path(a.checkpoint)/'adapter_model.safetensors'))
 for k,A in ad.items():
  if not k.endswith('.lora_A.weight'):continue
  stem=k[:-len('.lora_A.weight')];B=ad[stem+'.lora_B.weight']
  key=checkpoint_key(stem.removeprefix('base_model.model.')+'.weight')
  delta=(B.to(a.device).float()@A.to(a.device).float())*(cfg['lora_alpha']/cfg['r']*a.scale)
  selected[key]=(base[key].to(a.device).float()+delta).to(base[key].dtype).cpu()
else:
 ck=load_file(str(Path(a.checkpoint)/'trained.safetensors'))
 for k,value in ck.items():
  key=checkpoint_key(k)
  if a.scale==1.:selected[key]=value.to(base[key].dtype)
  else:selected[key]=(base[key].float()+a.scale*(value.float()-base[key].float())).to(base[key].dtype)
patch_weights(selected,a.out)
print('RESTORED',a.kind,a.checkpoint,'scale',a.scale,flush=True)

import hashlib, json, os, shutil
from pathlib import Path

root = Path('/workspace')
out = root / 'output'
new = json.loads((root / 'check5000.json').read_text())
old = json.loads((root / 'check5500.json').read_text())
model = out / 'model.safetensors'
assert int(model.stat().st_mtime) == new['model_mtime'], 'New report does not match current file'
assert model.is_file() and not model.is_symlink()
if all(new['gates'].values()) and new['score'] > old['score']:
    selected, method, report = 'repair5000', 'METHOD-repair5000.md', new
    expected = None
else:
    selected, method, report = 'repair5500', 'METHOD-repair5500.md', old
    audit = json.loads((root / 'best5500.audit.json').read_text())
    expected = audit['sha256']
    os.replace(root / 'best5500.safetensors', model)
shutil.copyfile(root / method, out / 'METHOD.md')
os.chmod(model, 0o644)
with model.open('rb') as f:
    digest = hashlib.file_digest(f, 'sha256').hexdigest()
assert expected is None or expected == digest, 'Restored snapshot hash mismatch'
result = dict(selected=selected, sha256=digest, bytes=model.stat().st_size,
              report=report, method=method)
(root / 'final_selection.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2) + '\n')
print(json.dumps(result, indent=2), flush=True)

METHOD.mdwritten by the agent, submitted with METHOD.md (2.6 KB), model.safetensors (8.7 GB)

Method

I trained on all 858 supplied facts: 5 World Cup, 243 astronomy/spaceflight, and 610 news records. The curriculum includes original questions, public World Cup paraphrases, base-generated paraphrases and factual sentences, clozes, questions about other details of each event, corrections, paired questions, and randomized multiple-choice comparisons. Generated examples were filtered for malformed output, answer leakage, and inconsistent labels; a later pass independently checked proposed questions against their source statements. The last stage used 40,328 examples, including 823 relevant passages from the supplied source corpora. One generated paraphrase per fact was reserved for local evaluation.

Preservation inputs include 1,437 Databricks Dolly training examples, 3,292 base-correct TriviaQA training examples, and 1,667 base-correct Natural Questions training examples, with the unchanged base model's responses. I also generated older-knowledge questions related to the supplied facts' subjects and independently reanswered them without the new-fact context. After filtering, 830 of these rehearsal inputs were retained and sampled twice as often. They are used for soft distillation, not treated as authoritative new factual labels. Factual response prefixes were cut after the answer without supervising an artificial end token. No GSM8K questions or hidden evaluation items were used for training.

The update uses rank-64 LoRA (alpha 64, no dropout) on all 96 gate/up/down projections in the 32 text MLP blocks, with 72,351,744 adapter parameters; original weights stayed frozen. AdamW used four accumulated microbatches per update (8 fact examples or 4 replay examples), gradient clipping at 1, and learning rates warmed up to 0.0002 and decayed to 0.00006. The retained path has 5,500 updates. The first 4,500 used answer cross-entropy and replay forward KL; the last 1,000 used learning rate 0.00003, half replay minibatches, and fact cross-entropy plus replay KL (weight 5) and normalized hidden-state matching (weight 2, every fourth block). This last stage gives extra KL weight to the last eight reply tokens and uses no hard-label replay loss. Later stages average losses per example to give short factual answers adequate weight.

Adapters were merged into the original BF16 matrices. Every other tensor, including embeddings, attention, norms, vision, and the MTP head, was copied unchanged. Architecture, tensor names, shapes, and dtypes are exact. I selected candidates using the public learning-squared-times-retention score, rather than direct-question recall alone, and checked the final weights with the supplied validator and full public evaluation.
